Create `internal/web/api.go`:

```go
package web

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/aidan-bailey/loom/core"
	"github.com/aidan-bailey/loom/log"
)

const (
	// maxReplyBytes is the longest reply a device may send a session, and
	// maxPromptBytes the longest first prompt of a new one.
	maxReplyBytes  = 64 << 10
	maxPromptBytes = 64 << 10
	// maxIDValue is the largest ID a request may name: IDs are JSON numbers,
	// exact in JavaScript up to 53 bits.
	maxIDValue = 1<<53 - 1
)

// noticesJSON is the body of an action that worked: the notices it raised.
type noticesJSON struct {
	Notices []noticeJSON `json:"notices"`
}

// createdJSON is the body of a create that worked.
type createdJSON struct {
	ID      core.InstanceID `json:"id"`
	Notices []noticeJSON    `json:"notices"`
}

// actionLog names one action for the log: who did what to which session.
type actionLog struct {
	device    string
	name      string
	sessionID core.InstanceID
}

// record logs the action with how it ended.
func (a actionLog) record(result string) {
	log.For("web").Info("web.action", "device", a.device, "action", a.name, "session", uint64(a.sessionID), "result", result)
}

// routeAPI registers the sessions' actions under /api/v1. Every route needs
// a paired device (authed); /pair, /stream and /push-key are other stories'.
func (l *Layer) routeAPI(m *http.ServeMux) {
	m.Handle("POST /api/v1/sessions/{id}/send", l.authed(l.sendReply))
	m.Handle("POST /api/v1/sessions/{id}/merge", l.authed(l.mergeInto))
	m.Handle("POST /api/v1/sessions/{id}/pause", l.authed(l.sessionOp("pause", core.Core.Pause)))
	m.Handle("POST /api/v1/sessions/{id}/resume", l.authed(l.sessionOp("resume", core.Core.Resume)))
	m.Handle("POST /api/v1/sessions/{id}/kill", l.authed(l.sessionOp("kill", core.Core.Kill)))
	m.Handle("POST /api/v1/sessions/{id}/push", l.authed(l.sessionOp("push", core.Core.Push)))
}

// modelStatus is the HTTP status a failed request earns: the session or the
// device is not there (404, 401), the daemon can't take the request (503), or
// the model refused or failed it, in words of its own (409).
func modelStatus(err error) int {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, core.ErrNoSession):
		return http.StatusNotFound
	case errors.Is(err, core.ErrNoDevice):
		return http.StatusUnauthorized
	case errors.Is(err, core.ErrUnavailable), errors.Is(err, core.ErrWebOff):
		return http.StatusServiceUnavailable
	}
	return http.StatusConflict
}

// decodeBody reads the request's JSON body into v, answering 400 when it is
// not that (an unknown field included).
func decodeBody(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, v any) bool {
	if err := readJSON(r, v); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad request: "+err.Error())
		return false
	}
	return true
}

// sessionFromPath is the session the path names, answered 404 when the path
// holds no decimal ID the model serves.
func (l *Layer) sessionFromPath(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) (core.InstanceID, bool) {
	n, err := strconv.ParseUint(r.PathValue("id"), 10, 64)
	if err == nil && n != 0 && n <= maxIDValue {
		if _, ok := l.client.View(core.InstanceID(n)); ok {
			return core.InstanceID(n), true
		}
	}
	writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "no such session")
	return 0, false
}

// doAction makes the request send names, with the web client's next request
// ID, and answers from how it ended.
func (l *Layer) doAction(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, a actionLog, send func(req core.ReqID)) {
	req := l.reqs.next()
	send(req)
	reply, notes, done := l.reqs.wait(r.Context(), req, a.device)
	l.writeOutcome(w, a, reply, notes, done, false)
}

// writeOutcome answers an action from how its request ended. Not done is the
// wait running out: 202, the outcome following on the stream. An error in the
// Reply keeps the model's text with the status it earns, and any notice
// beside it goes to the device's stream; otherwise the notices ride the
// response, with the new session's ID for a create.
func (l *Layer) writeOutcome(w http.ResponseWriter, a actionLog, reply core.Reply, notes []noticeJSON, done, created bool) {
	switch {
	case !done:
		a.record("pending")
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusAccepted, map[string]bool{"pending": true})
	case reply.Err != nil:
		text := reply.Err.Error()
		for _, n := range notes {
			if n.Text != text {
				l.reqs.stream(a.device, n)
			}
		}
		status := modelStatus(reply.Err)
		a.record(fmt.Sprintf("%d %s", status, text))
		writeError(w, status, text)
	case created:
		a.sessionID = reply.ID
		a.record("created")
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusCreated, createdJSON{ID: reply.ID, Notices: nonNil(notes)})
	default:
		a.record("ok")
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, noticesJSON{Notices: nonNil(notes)})
	}
}

// nonNil is notes, as an empty list rather than null when there are none.
func nonNil(notes []noticeJSON) []noticeJSON {
	if notes == nil {
		return []noticeJSON{}
	}
	return notes
}

// sessionOp is the handler of a lifecycle request that names only the
// session: pause, resume, kill and push.
func (l *Layer) sessionOp(name string, op func(core.Core, core.InstanceID, core.ReqID)) deviceHandler {
	return func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, device string) {
		id, ok := l.sessionFromPath(w, r)
		if !ok {
			return
		}
		l.doAction(w, r, actionLog{device: device, name: name, sessionID: id}, func(req core.ReqID) {
			op(l.client, id, req)
		})
	}
}

// sendBody is POST /api/v1/sessions/{id}/send.
type sendBody struct {
	Text string `json:"text"`
}

// sendReply sends the device's reply to the session through the loom mod
// (core.OriginWeb: the person's own words, no "From" line).
func (l *Layer) sendReply(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, device string) {
	id, ok := l.sessionFromPath(w, r)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	var body sendBody
	if !decodeBody(w, r, &body) {
		return
	}
	text := strings.TrimSpace(body.Text)
	if text == "" || len(text) > maxReplyBytes {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, fmt.Sprintf("text: want 1 to %d bytes", maxReplyBytes))
		return
	}
	l.doAction(w, r, actionLog{device: device, name: "send", sessionID: id}, func(req core.ReqID) {
		l.client.Send(id, core.Message{Text: text, Origin: core.OriginWeb}, req)
	})
}

// mergeBody is POST /api/v1/sessions/{id}/merge.
type mergeBody struct {
	Source core.InstanceID `json:"source"`
}

// mergeInto merges the source session into the one in the URL, as the TUI's
// picker does; the model checks both (admit).
func (l *Layer) mergeInto(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, device string) {
	id, ok := l.sessionFromPath(w, r)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	var body mergeBody
	if !decodeBody(w, r, &body) {
		return
	}
	if body.Source == 0 || body.Source > maxIDValue {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "source: want a session ID")
		return
	}
	if _, ok := l.client.View(body.Source); !ok {
		writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "source: no such session")
		return
	}
	l.doAction(w, r, actionLog{device: device, name: "merge", sessionID: id}, func(req core.ReqID) {
		l.client.Merge(id, body.Source, req)
	})
}
```

```go
func TestAPI_CreateAndLaunchDefaultsNeedADevice(t *testing.T) {
	env := newAPIEnv(t, apiFixture{})

	status, _, _ := env.send(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/sessions", map[string]any{"workspace": env.ws, "title": "x"}, false)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, status)
	status, _, _ = env.send(http.MethodGet, fmt.Sprintf("/api/v1/launch-defaults?workspace=%d", env.ws), nil, false)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, status)
	assert.Empty(t, env.layer.client.Views(env.ws), "nothing was created")
}

func TestAPI_CreateStartsASessionWithTheChosenOptions(t *testing.T) {
	env := newAPIEnv(t, apiFixture{})

	status, out := env.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/sessions", map[string]any{
		"workspace": env.ws, "title": " fresh ", "prompt": "hello\n\n",
		"model": "opus", "permission_mode": "plan", "effort": "high",
	})

	require.Equal(t, http.StatusCreated, status, string(out))
	var got struct {
		ID      core.InstanceID `json:"id"`
		Notices []noticeJSON    `json:"notices"`
	}
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(out, &got))
	assert.NotZero(t, got.ID)
	assert.NotNil(t, got.Notices, "a list, even an empty one")
	v := env.view(got.ID)
	assert.Equal(t, "fresh", v.Title, "the title is trimmed")
	assert.Equal(t, session.Loading, v.Status, "the start plays out on the stream")
	opts, _ := launch.Parse(v.Program)
	assert.Equal(t, "opus", opts.Model)
	assert.Equal(t, "plan", opts.PermissionMode)
	assert.Equal(t, "high", opts.Effort)
}

func TestAPI_CreateRefusesWhatLoomSessionNewRefuses(t *testing.T) {
	env := newAPIEnv(t, apiFixture{insts: []*session.Instance{apiInstance(t, "taken", session.Running)}})
	ws := env.ws

	for _, c := range []struct {
		name   string
		body   any
		status int
		want   string
	}{
		{"no workspace", map[string]any{"title": "x"}, http.StatusBadRequest, "workspace: no such workspace"},
		{"an unknown workspace", map[string]any{"workspace": 424242, "title": "x"}, http.StatusBadRequest, "workspace: no such workspace"},
		{"a blank title", map[string]any{"workspace": ws, "title": "  "}, http.StatusBadRequest, "title cannot be empty"},
		{"a title too wide", map[string]any{"workspace": ws, "title": strings.Repeat("w", core.MaxTitleWidth+1)}, http.StatusBadRequest, "title cannot be longer than"},
		{"a title the workspace holds", map[string]any{"workspace": ws, "title": "taken"}, http.StatusConflict, `already has a session titled "taken"`},
		{"a model Claude lacks", map[string]any{"workspace": ws, "title": "x", "model": "gpt"}, http.StatusBadRequest, `model "gpt": want one of`},
		{"a permission mode it lacks", map[string]any{"workspace": ws, "title": "x", "permission_mode": "yolo"}, http.StatusBadRequest, `permission_mode "yolo": want one of`},
		{"an effort it lacks", map[string]any{"workspace": ws, "title": "x", "effort": "extreme"}, http.StatusBadRequest, `effort "extreme": want one of`},
		{"an unregistered account", map[string]any{"workspace": ws, "title": "x", "account": "ghost"}, http.StatusBadRequest, `account "ghost" is not registered`},
		{"a prompt over 64 KiB", map[string]any{"workspace": ws, "title": "x", "prompt": strings.Repeat("p", 64<<10+1)}, http.StatusBadRequest, "prompt: want at most"},
		{"a field it does not know", map[string]any{"workspace": ws, "title": "x", "branch": "main"}, http.StatusBadRequest, "unknown field"},
	} {
		status, out := env.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/sessions", c.body)
		assert.Equal(t, c.status, status, c.name)
		assert.Contains(t, apiErrorOf(t, out), c.want, c.name)
	}
	assert.Len(t, env.layer.client.Views(ws), 1, "only the session that was there")
}

// apiDefaults is the body of GET /api/v1/launch-defaults.
type apiDefaults struct {
	Account         string   `json:"account"`
	Accounts        []string `json:"accounts"`
	Model           string   `json:"model"`
	Models          []string `json:"models"`
	PermissionMode  string   `json:"permission_mode"`
	PermissionModes []string `json:"permission_modes"`
	Effort          string   `json:"effort"`
	Efforts         []string `json:"efforts"`
}

// defaults reads the launch defaults of the fixture's workspace.
func (e *apiEnv) defaults() apiDefaults {
	e.t.Helper()
	status, out := e.do(http.MethodGet, fmt.Sprintf("/api/v1/launch-defaults?workspace=%d", e.ws), nil)
	require.Equal(e.t, http.StatusOK, status, string(out))
	var got apiDefaults
	require.NoError(e.t, json.Unmarshal(out, &got))
	return got
}

func TestAPI_LaunchDefaultsFollowTheWorkspace(t *testing.T) {
	env := newAPIEnv(t, apiFixture{workspaceConfig: `{"default_program":"claude","claude_model":"sonnet","claude_permission_mode":"plan","claude_effort":"high"}`})
	names := env.layer.client.AccountNames()
	wantAccounts, wantAccount := []string{}, ""
	if names.Present {
		wantAccounts, wantAccount = names.Names, names.Default
	}

	got := env.defaults()

	assert.Equal(t, "sonnet", got.Model)
	assert.Equal(t, "plan", got.PermissionMode)
	assert.Equal(t, "high", got.Effort)
	assert.Equal(t, wantAccount, got.Account)
	assert.NotNil(t, got.Accounts, "a list, even an empty one")
	assert.ElementsMatch(t, wantAccounts, got.Accounts)
	assert.Equal(t, config.ClaudeModelAliases(), got.Models, "the app hard-codes none of the lists")
	assert.Equal(t, config.ClaudePermissionModes, got.PermissionModes)
	assert.Equal(t, config.ClaudeEfforts, got.Efforts)
}

func TestAPI_LaunchDefaultsOfAProgramThatIsNotClaudeOfferNoAccounts(t *testing.T) {
	env := newAPIEnv(t, apiFixture{workspaceConfig: `{"default_program":"aider"}`})

	got := env.defaults()

	assert.Equal(t, "", got.Account)
	assert.NotNil(t, got.Accounts, "a list, even an empty one")
	assert.Empty(t, got.Accounts, "the app offers no account picker for it")
	assert.Equal(t, "default", got.Model)
	assert.Equal(t, "default", got.PermissionMode)
	assert.Equal(t, "default", got.Effort)
}

func TestAPI_LaunchDefaultsNeedAWorkspace(t *testing.T) {
	env := newAPIEnv(t, apiFixture{})

	for _, query := range []string{"", "?workspace=abc", "?workspace=0", "?workspace=424242"} {
		status, out := env.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/launch-defaults"+query, nil)
		assert.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, status, query)
		assert.Equal(t, "workspace: no such workspace", apiErrorOf(t, out), query)
	}
}
```

Append to the end of `internal/web/api.go`:

```go
// statusError is a refusal that knows its HTTP status.
type statusError struct {
	status int
	msg    string
}

// createBody is POST /api/v1/sessions: the overrides are optional.
type createBody struct {
	Workspace      core.WorkspaceID `json:"workspace"`
	Title          string           `json:"title"`
	Prompt         string           `json:"prompt"`
	Account        string           `json:"account"`
	Model          string           `json:"model"`
	PermissionMode string           `json:"permission_mode"`
	Effort         string           `json:"effort"`
}

// createSession creates and starts a session as `loom session new` does: the
// checks the TUI makes (core's), a title no row of the workspace holds, then
// the launch options (launchOptions), then the Create, whose Reply names the
// new session at once. The start plays out on the stream.
func (l *Layer) createSession(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, device string) {
	var body createBody
	if !decodeBody(w, r, &body) {
		return
	}
	title := strings.TrimSpace(body.Title)
	prompt := strings.TrimRight(body.Prompt, " \t\r\n")
	if len(prompt) > maxPromptBytes {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, fmt.Sprintf("prompt: want at most %d bytes", maxPromptBytes))
		return
	}
	ws, ok := l.client.Workspace(body.Workspace)
	if !ok {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "workspace: no such workspace")
		return
	}
	if err := core.CheckNewTitle(title, ws); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, err.Error())
		return
	}
	views := l.client.Views(ws.ID)
	for _, check := range []error{core.CheckRoom(len(views)), core.CheckWritable(ws)} {
		if check != nil {
			writeError(w, http.StatusConflict, check.Error())
			return
		}
	}
	for _, v := range views {
		if v.Title == title {
			writeError(w, http.StatusConflict, fmt.Sprintf("%s already has a session titled %q", ws.Label, title))
			return
		}
	}
	program := ws.Settings.GetProgram()
	l.client.ReloadAccounts()
	opts, notice, refused := l.launchOptions(ws, program, body)
	if refused != nil {
		writeError(w, refused.status, refused.msg)
		return
	}
	var pre []noticeJSON
	if notice != "" {
		pre = append(pre, noticeOf(core.Notice{Info: notice}, l.now()))
	}
	req := l.reqs.next()
	l.client.Create(ws.ID, core.NewInstance{
		Title: title, Path: ws.RepoPath, Program: program, Prompt: prompt, Launch: opts, Start: true,
	}, req)
	reply, notes, done := l.reqs.wait(r.Context(), req, device)
	l.writeOutcome(w, actionLog{device: device, name: "create"}, reply, append(pre, notes...), done, true)
}

// launchOptions are the options a new session in ws launches program with:
// what the Session Launch Options modal pre-fills (the workspace's settings,
// then core.ChooseLaunchAccount), then body's overrides. The caller reloads
// the accounts first. The notice is the modal's, when the launch moved off a
// removed account. Remote control the account's login can't use is refused,
// as `loom session new` refuses it: the app has no flag to start without it,
// so the message names the workspace's setting.
func (l *Layer) launchOptions(ws core.WorkspaceView, program string, body createBody) (launch.Options, string, *statusError) {
	for _, o := range []struct {
		name, value string
		allowed     []string
	}{
		{"model", body.Model, config.ClaudeModelAliases()},
		{"permission_mode", body.PermissionMode, config.ClaudePermissionModes},
		{"effort", body.Effort, config.ClaudeEfforts},
	} {
		if o.value != "" && !slices.Contains(o.allowed, o.value) {
			return launch.Options{}, "", &statusError{http.StatusBadRequest, fmt.Sprintf("%s %q: want one of %s", o.name, o.value, strings.Join(o.allowed, ", "))}
		}
	}
	opts, notice := core.ChooseLaunchAccount(l.client, launch.FromSettings(ws.Settings), program)
	if body.Account != "" {
		switch {
		case !session.IsClaudeProgram(program):
			return launch.Options{}, "", &statusError{http.StatusBadRequest, fmt.Sprintf("account: %s is not Claude", program)}
		case body.Account != account.DefaultName:
			if _, ok := l.client.Account(body.Account); !ok {
				return launch.Options{}, "", &statusError{http.StatusBadRequest, fmt.Sprintf("account %q is not registered", body.Account)}
			}
		}
		opts.Account = body.Account
	}
	if body.Model != "" {
		opts.Model = body.Model
	}
	if body.PermissionMode != "" {
		opts.PermissionMode = body.PermissionMode
	}
	if body.Effort != "" {
		opts.Effort = body.Effort
	}
	if auth := l.client.RCAuthFor(opts.Account); launch.RemoteControlBlocked(auth, launch.EffectiveRemoteControl(opts), program) {
		return launch.Options{}, "", &statusError{http.StatusConflict, fmt.Sprintf("remote control is unavailable: %s (turn it off in the workspace's settings to start without it)", auth.Reason)}
	}
	return opts, notice, nil
}

// launchDefaultsJSON is what the new-session sheet pre-fills, the account
// names it offers and the lists it picks from.
type launchDefaultsJSON struct {
	Account         string   `json:"account"`
	Accounts        []string `json:"accounts"`
	Model           string   `json:"model"`
	Models          []string `json:"models"`
	PermissionMode  string   `json:"permission_mode"`
	PermissionModes []string `json:"permission_modes"`
	Effort          string   `json:"effort"`
	Efforts         []string `json:"efforts"`
}

// launchDefaults answers GET /api/v1/launch-defaults?workspace=<id> as the
// modal would pre-fill a new session in that workspace. The accounts are the
// registry's, for a Claude program only: any other takes no account.
func (l *Layer) launchDefaults(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, _ string) {
	n, err := strconv.ParseUint(r.URL.Query().Get("workspace"), 10, 64)
	ws, ok := l.client.Workspace(core.WorkspaceID(n))
	if err != nil || !ok {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "workspace: no such workspace")
		return
	}
	program := ws.Settings.GetProgram()
	l.client.ReloadAccounts()
	opts, _ := core.ChooseLaunchAccount(l.client, launch.FromSettings(ws.Settings), program)
	out := launchDefaultsJSON{
		Account:         opts.Account,
		Accounts:        []string{},
		Model:           opts.Model,
		Models:          config.ClaudeModelAliases(),
		PermissionMode:  opts.PermissionMode,
		PermissionModes: slices.Clone(config.ClaudePermissionModes),
		Effort:          opts.Effort,
		Efforts:         slices.Clone(config.ClaudeEfforts),
	}
	if names := l.client.AccountNames(); names.Present && session.IsClaudeProgram(program) {
		out.Accounts = append(out.Accounts, names.Names...)
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, out)
}
```

```go
func TestAPI_PutDeviceChangesOnlyWhatItNames(t *testing.T) {
	env := newAPIEnv(t, apiFixture{})
	before := env.notify()

	status, out := env.do(http.MethodPut, "/api/v1/device", map[string]any{"waiting": !before.Waiting})
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(out))
	var dev struct {
		Name string `json:"name"`
	}
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(out, &dev))
	assert.Equal(t, "phone", dev.Name)
	after := env.notify()
	assert.Equal(t, !before.Waiting, after.Waiting)
	assert.Equal(t, before.Finished, after.Finished, "a setting left out stays")
	assert.True(t, after.PausedUntil.IsZero())

	// Pausing keeps the settings it does not name.
	start := time.Now()
	status, out = env.do(http.MethodPut, "/api/v1/device", map[string]any{"pause": "1h"})
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(out))
	paused := env.notify()
	assert.Equal(t, !before.Waiting, paused.Waiting)
	assert.WithinDuration(t, start.Add(time.Hour), paused.PausedUntil, time.Minute)

	status, out = env.do(http.MethodPut, "/api/v1/device", map[string]any{"pause": "none"})
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(out))
	assert.True(t, env.notify().PausedUntil.IsZero())
}

// apiSubscription is a push subscription body in the shape of the browser's
// PushSubscription.toJSON(), with a real P-256 point, its keys encoded by enc.
func apiSubscription(t *testing.T, endpoint string, enc *base64.Encoding) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	key, err := ecdh.P256().GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	auth := make([]byte, 16)
	_, err = rand.Read(auth)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return map[string]any{
		"endpoint":       endpoint,
		"expirationTime": nil,
		"keys": map[string]string{
			"p256dh": enc.EncodeToString(key.PublicKey().Bytes()),
			"auth":   enc.EncodeToString(auth),
		},
	}
}

func TestAPI_PostPushStoresTheSubscription(t *testing.T) {
	env := newAPIEnv(t, apiFixture{})
	devicePush := func() core.PushState {
		for _, d := range env.loop.Web().Devices {
			if d.Name == "phone" {
				return d.Push
			}
		}
		return "no device"
	}
	require.Equal(t, core.PushNone, devicePush())
	const apple = "https://web.push.apple.com/push/1"

	// A host the daemon would not post to, and a key of the wrong size, are
	// refused before the model hears of them.
	status, out := env.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/device/push", apiSubscription(t, "https://evil.example.com/push/1", base64.RawURLEncoding))
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, status)
	assert.NotEmpty(t, apiErrorOf(t, out))
	short := apiSubscription(t, apple, base64.RawURLEncoding)
	short["keys"] = map[string]string{"p256dh": "AAAA", "auth": "AAAA"}
	status, out = env.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/device/push", short)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, status)
	assert.NotEmpty(t, apiErrorOf(t, out))
	assert.Equal(t, core.PushNone, devicePush(), "refused: nothing stored")

	// Keys with their padding are taken: the model takes only the unpadded.
	status, out = env.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/device/push", apiSubscription(t, apple, base64.URLEncoding))
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(out))
	var dev struct {
		Name string `json:"name"`
	}
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(out, &dev))
	assert.Equal(t, "phone", dev.Name)
	assert.Equal(t, core.PushOK, devicePush())

	status, out = env.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/device/push", apiSubscription(t, apple, base64.RawURLEncoding))
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(out))
}

func TestAPI_DeletePushDropsTheSubscription(t *testing.T) {
	env := newAPIEnv(t, apiFixture{})
	devicePush := func() core.PushState {
		for _, d := range env.loop.Web().Devices {
			if d.Name == "phone" {
				return d.Push
			}
		}
		return "no device"
	}

	// With no subscription there is nothing to drop: the state stays none.
	status, out := env.do(http.MethodDelete, "/api/v1/device/push", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, status, string(out))
	assert.Equal(t, core.PushNone, devicePush(), "nothing to expire")

	status, out = env.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/device/push", apiSubscription(t, "https://web.push.apple.com/push/1", base64.RawURLEncoding))
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(out))
	require.Equal(t, core.PushOK, devicePush())
	require.Len(t, env.loop.PushTargets(), 1, "control: the sender would push to it")

	status, out = env.do(http.MethodDelete, "/api/v1/device/push", nil)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, status, string(out))
	assert.Equal(t, core.PushExpired, devicePush(), "the app shows notifications to turn on again")
	assert.Empty(t, env.loop.PushTargets(), "nothing is pushed to the dropped subscription")
	assert.Len(t, env.loop.Web().Devices, 1, "the phone stays paired")
}

func TestAPI_DeleteDeviceUnpairsThePhone(t *testing.T) {
	env := newAPIEnv(t, apiFixture{})

	status, out, header := env.send(http.MethodDelete, "/api/v1/device", nil, true)

	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, status, string(out))
	cleared := false
	for _, c := range (&http.Response{Header: header}).Cookies() {
		if c.Name == cookieName {
			cleared = true
			assert.Negative(t, c.MaxAge, "the cookie is expired on the phone")
		}
	}
	assert.True(t, cleared, "the response clears the device cookie")
	assert.Empty(t, env.loop.Web().Devices)
	status, _ = env.do(http.MethodPut, "/api/v1/device", map[string]any{"waiting": true})
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, status, "the token no longer names a device")
	status, _ = env.do(http.MethodDelete, "/api/v1/device", nil)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, status, "and unpairing twice is refused, not an error")
}
```

```go
// deviceBody is PUT /api/v1/device: the notification settings to change; one
// left out stays as it is. Pause is "1h" (an hour from now) or "none".
type deviceBody struct {
	Waiting  *bool   `json:"waiting"`
	Finished *bool   `json:"finished"`
	Pause    *string `json:"pause"`
}

// putDevice changes the device's notification settings, only the ones the
// body names (core.NotifyChange), and answers the device as the model stored
// it.
func (l *Layer) putDevice(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, device string) {
	a := actionLog{device: device, name: "notify"}
	var body deviceBody
	if !decodeBody(w, r, &body) {
		return
	}
	change := core.NotifyChange{Waiting: body.Waiting, Finished: body.Finished}
	if body.Pause != nil {
		switch *body.Pause {
		case "1h":
			until := l.now().Add(time.Hour)
			change.PausedUntil = &until
		case "none":
			change.PausedUntil = &time.Time{}
		default:
			writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, `pause: want "1h" or "none"`)
			return
		}
	}
	stored, err := l.host.SetNotify(device, change)
	if err != nil {
		a.record(err.Error())
		writeError(w, modelStatus(err), err.Error())
		return
	}
	a.record("ok")
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, deviceOf(stored))
}

// subscriptionKeys are a subscription's keys, base64url as the browser gives them.
type subscriptionKeys struct {
	P256DH string `json:"p256dh"`
	Auth   string `json:"auth"`
}

// subscriptionBody is POST /api/v1/device/push, in the shape of the browser's
// PushSubscription.toJSON(). Its expirationTime is read and ignored.
type subscriptionBody struct {
	Endpoint       string           `json:"endpoint"`
	ExpirationTime any              `json:"expirationTime"`
	Keys           subscriptionKeys `json:"keys"`
}

// postPush stores the device's push subscription, refused when it names a
// host the daemon would not post to or keys of the wrong size
// (core.ValidatePushSubscription), and answers the device. Padding is
// stripped from the keys first.
func (l *Layer) postPush(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, device string) {
	a := actionLog{device: device, name: "subscribe"}
	var body subscriptionBody
	if !decodeBody(w, r, &body) {
		return
	}
	// The model takes unpadded base64url only; a browser may give padding.
	sub := core.PushSubscription{
		Endpoint: body.Endpoint,
		P256DH:   strings.TrimRight(body.Keys.P256DH, "="),
		Auth:     strings.TrimRight(body.Keys.Auth, "="),
	}
	if err := core.ValidatePushSubscription(sub); err != nil {
		a.record("400 " + err.Error())
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, err.Error())
		return
	}
	stored, err := l.host.SetPushSubscription(device, sub)
	if err != nil {
		a.record(err.Error())
		writeError(w, modelStatus(err), err.Error())
		return
	}
	a.record("ok")
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, deviceOf(stored))
}

// deletePush drops the device's push subscription, for the app's launch
// check: Apple answers 201 to a subscription the phone has dropped, and drops
// the push, so the sender never learns of it (spec, "Push"). The model
// records the state as expired, as for a 410, which the app shows as
// notifications to turn on again; a device with no subscription is left as it
// is. It answers 204.
func (l *Layer) deletePush(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request, device string) {
	a := actionLog{device: device, name: "unsubscribe"}
	l.host.PushResult(device, core.PushExpired)
	a.record("ok")
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
}

// deleteDevice unpairs the phone that asks: the model forgets the device
// (which ends its streams and drops its subscription) and the cookie is
// cleared. A device already gone is a device unpaired.
func (l *Layer) deleteDevice(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request, device string) {
	a := actionLog{device: device, name: "unpair"}
	if err := l.client.RevokeDevice(device); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, core.ErrNoDevice) {
		a.record(err.Error())
		writeError(w, modelStatus(err), err.Error())
		return
	}
	a.record("ok")
	clearDeviceCookie(w)
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

Create `e2e/web_test.go`. It runs a sandbox daemon with the web enabled on a loopback port, pairs a device with `loom web`, opens the stream, and drives a fake agent's session over HTTP as the app does:

```go
//go:build e2e

package e2e

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/aidan-bailey/loom/internal/devsandbox"
	"github.com/aidan-bailey/loom/session/tmux"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

// The web layer end to end (the loom PWA): a sandbox daemon with the web
// enabled pairs a device, streams the fleet to it, and creates, pauses,
// resumes and kills a fake agent's session over HTTP, as the phone's app
// does.

// pairingCode matches the code `loom web pair` prints, XXXX-XXXX.
var pairingCode = regexp.MustCompile(`[2-9A-HJKMNP-Z]{4}-[2-9A-HJKMNP-Z]{4}`)

// webCookie is the device cookie's name (cookieName in internal/web).
const webCookie = "__Host-loom"

// sseEvent is one server-sent event of the stream.
type sseEvent struct {
	Name string
	Data string
}

// readStream parses a server-sent event stream into events until it ends or
// ctx is done.
func readStream(ctx context.Context, body io.Reader) <-chan sseEvent {
	out := make(chan sseEvent, 64)
	go func() {
		defer close(out)
		sc := bufio.NewScanner(body)
		sc.Buffer(make([]byte, 0, 64<<10), 1<<20)
		var ev sseEvent
		for sc.Scan() {
			line := sc.Text()
			switch {
			case line == "":
				if ev.Name != "" {
					select {
					case out <- ev:
					case <-ctx.Done():
						return
					}
				}
				ev = sseEvent{}
			case strings.HasPrefix(line, "event:"):
				ev.Name = strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "event:"))
			case strings.HasPrefix(line, "data:"):
				ev.Data += strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "data:"))
			}
		}
	}()
	return out
}

// awaitEvent reads the stream until an event named name that match accepts
// (nil: any) arrives, and returns it. The stream ending, or uiTimeout, fails
// the test.
func awaitEvent(t *testing.T, events <-chan sseEvent, name string, match func(sseEvent) bool) sseEvent {
	t.Helper()
	timeout := time.After(uiTimeout)
	for {
		select {
		case ev, ok := <-events:
			if !ok {
				t.Fatalf("the stream ended while waiting for a %q event", name)
			}
			if ev.Name == name && (match == nil || match(ev)) {
				return ev
			}
		case <-timeout:
			t.Fatalf("no %q event arrived within %s", name, uiTimeout)
		}
	}
}

// awaitEnd reads the stream until it ends.
func awaitEnd(t *testing.T, events <-chan sseEvent) {
	t.Helper()
	timeout := time.After(uiTimeout)
	for {
		select {
		case _, ok := <-events:
			if !ok {
				return
			}
		case <-timeout:
			t.Fatalf("the stream did not end within %s", uiTimeout)
		}
	}
}

// sessionsListing matches a `sessions` event of workspace ws, whose list
// holds a session titled title when want is true (and ok accepts its label,
// when ok is not nil), or none when want is false.
func sessionsListing(ws uint64, title string, want bool, ok func(label string) bool) func(sseEvent) bool {
	return func(ev sseEvent) bool {
		var got struct {
			Workspace uint64 `json:"workspace"`
			Sessions  []struct {
				Title string `json:"title"`
				Label string `json:"label"`
			} `json:"sessions"`
		}
		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(ev.Data), &got) != nil || got.Workspace != ws {
			return false
		}
		for _, s := range got.Sessions {
			if s.Title == title {
				return want && (ok == nil || ok(s.Label))
			}
		}
		return !want
	}
}

// webClient talks to a sandbox daemon's web layer as a phone does.
type webClient struct {
	t      *testing.T
	origin string
	token  string
	client *http.Client
}

// request builds a request to the layer; a non-GET carries the Origin the
// layer demands.
func (c *webClient) request(method, path string, body any) *http.Request {
	c.t.Helper()
	var rd io.Reader
	if body != nil {
		raw, err := json.Marshal(body)
		require.NoError(c.t, err)
		rd = bytes.NewReader(raw)
	}
	req, err := http.NewRequest(method, c.origin+path, rd)
	require.NoError(c.t, err)
	if method != http.MethodGet {
		req.Header.Set("Origin", c.origin)
		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	}
	return req
}

// round sends req and returns the status, the body and the headers.
func (c *webClient) round(req *http.Request) (int, []byte, http.Header) {
	c.t.Helper()
	resp, err := c.client.Do(req)
	require.NoError(c.t, err)
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	out, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	require.NoError(c.t, err)
	return resp.StatusCode, out, resp.Header
}

// call makes the request as the paired device.
func (c *webClient) call(method, path string, body any) (int, []byte) {
	c.t.Helper()
	req := c.request(method, path, body)
	req.AddCookie(&http.Cookie{Name: webCookie, Value: c.token})
	status, out, _ := c.round(req)
	return status, out
}

// freeLoopbackAddr is a loopback address nothing listens on yet.
func freeLoopbackAddr(t *testing.T) string {
	t.Helper()
	ln, err := net.Listen("tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	addr := ln.Addr().String()
	require.NoError(t, ln.Close())
	return addr
}

// `loom web enable` starts the layer in the sandbox's daemon, `loom web
// pair` makes a code the phone posts for its cookie, and the cookie opens
// the stream and the routes. A session is created, paused, resumed and
// killed over HTTP; the agent's tmux session and the stream's lists follow
// each step. `loom web revoke` ends the stream and the cookie.
func TestE2E_Web_SessionLifecycleOverHTTP(t *testing.T) {
	sb := newSandbox(t, "")
	addr := freeLoopbackAddr(t)
	web := &webClient{t: t, origin: "http://" + addr, client: &http.Client{Timeout: 45 * time.Second}}
	loomWeb := func(args ...string) string {
		t.Helper()
		out, err := runLoom(t, sb, append([]string{sb.LoomBin(), "web"}, args...)...)
		require.NoError(t, err, out)
		return out
	}

	loomWeb("enable", "--listen", addr, "--origin", web.origin)
	require.Eventually(t, func() bool {
		conn, err := net.DialTimeout("tcp", addr, time.Second)
		if err != nil {
			return false
		}
		_ = conn.Close()
		return true
	}, uiTimeout, 100*time.Millisecond, "the daemon never listened on %s", addr)

	// The guard, before any device is paired.
	status, _, _ := web.round(web.request(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/launch-defaults?workspace=1", nil))
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, status, "an API route needs the cookie")
	wrongHost := web.request(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/launch-defaults?workspace=1", nil)
	wrongHost.Host = "evil.example.com"
	status, _, _ = web.round(wrongHost)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusMisdirectedRequest, status, "a Host that is not the origin's is refused")

	// Pairing: the code posted alone gives the cookie.
	code := pairingCode.FindString(loomWeb("pair", "--name", "e2e"))
	require.NotEmpty(t, code, "loom web pair printed no code")
	status, body, header := web.round(web.request(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/pair", map[string]string{"code": code}))
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(body))
	for _, c := range (&http.Response{Header: header}).Cookies() {
		if c.Name == webCookie {
			web.token = c.Value
		}
	}
	require.NotEmpty(t, web.token, "pairing sets the device cookie")
	crossOrigin := web.request(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/sessions/1/kill", nil)
	crossOrigin.Header.Set("Origin", "https://evil.example.com")
	crossOrigin.AddCookie(&http.Cookie{Name: webCookie, Value: web.token})
	status, _, _ = web.round(crossOrigin)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusForbidden, status, "an Origin that is not the origin is refused, cookie or not")

	// The stream: a snapshot names the sandbox's workspace.
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	t.Cleanup(cancel)
	streamReq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, web.origin+"/api/v1/stream", nil)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	streamReq.AddCookie(&http.Cookie{Name: webCookie, Value: web.token})
	resp, err := (&http.Client{}).Do(streamReq)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() })
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, resp.StatusCode)
	events := readStream(ctx, resp.Body)
	snapshot := awaitEvent(t, events, "snapshot", nil)
	var fleet struct {
		Workspaces []struct {
			ID   uint64 `json:"id"`
			Name string `json:"name"`
		} `json:"workspaces"`
	}
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal([]byte(snapshot.Data), &fleet))
	var ws uint64
	for _, w := range fleet.Workspaces {
		if w.Name == devsandbox.WorkspaceName {
			ws = w.ID
		}
	}
	require.NotZero(t, ws, "the snapshot lists the workspace %s: %s", devsandbox.WorkspaceName, snapshot.Data)

	// Create: answered at once with the new ID; the start follows on the
	// stream and in tmux.
	status, body = web.call(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/sessions", map[string]any{"workspace": ws, "title": "webkeeper"})
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusCreated, status, string(body))
	var created struct {
		ID      uint64 `json:"id"`
		Notices []any  `json:"notices"`
	}
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(body, &created))
	require.NotZero(t, created.ID)
	awaitEvent(t, events, "sessions", sessionsListing(ws, "webkeeper", true, nil))
	require.Eventually(t, func() bool { return hasSession(sb, "webkeeper") }, uiTimeout, 100*time.Millisecond, "the agent's tmux session never started")
	require.Eventually(t, func() bool {
		return strings.Contains(paneText(t, sb, tmux.ToLoomTmuxName("webkeeper")), "commands: work N")
	}, uiTimeout, 100*time.Millisecond, "the fake agent came up")

	// Pause, resume, kill: each answered when the model has done it.
	path := fmt.Sprintf("/api/v1/sessions/%d/", created.ID)
	status, body = web.call(http.MethodPost, path+"pause", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(body))
	assert.False(t, hasSession(sb, "webkeeper"), "pausing ended the agent's tmux session")
	awaitEvent(t, events, "sessions", sessionsListing(ws, "webkeeper", true, func(label string) bool { return label == "Paused" }))

	status, body = web.call(http.MethodPost, path+"resume", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(body))
	require.Eventually(t, func() bool { return hasSession(sb, "webkeeper") }, uiTimeout, 100*time.Millisecond, "resuming never started the agent")

	status, body = web.call(http.MethodPost, path+"kill", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(body))
	assert.False(t, hasSession(sb, "webkeeper"), "killing ended the agent's tmux session")
	awaitEvent(t, events, "sessions", sessionsListing(ws, "webkeeper", false, nil))
	status, body = web.call(http.MethodPost, path+"kill", nil)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, status, "the session is gone: %s", body)

	// Revoking the device ends its stream and its cookie.
	loomWeb("revoke", "e2e")
	awaitEnd(t, events)
	status, _ = web.call(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/launch-defaults?workspace=1", nil)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, status, "the revoked token names no device")
}
```

## Story 9: The app's shell: serving it, the service worker, the manifest and icons, install and pairing

- **Developer:** intermediate-dev
- **Files:** `internal/web/static.go`, `internal/web/static_test.go`, `internal/web/web.go`, `internal/web/static/index.html`, `internal/web/static/app.css`, `internal/web/static/sw.js`, `internal/web/static/manifest.webmanifest`, `internal/web/static/lib/preact-htm.mjs`, `internal/web/static/js/app.mjs`, `internal/web/static/js/api.mjs`, `internal/web/static/js/store.mjs`, `internal/web/static/js/pair.mjs`, `internal/web/static/js/screens.mjs`, `internal/web/static/icons/icon-180.png`, `internal/web/static/icons/icon-192.png`, `internal/web/static/icons/icon-512.png`, `tools/webicons/main.go`, `tools/CLAUDE.md`, `NOTICE.md`
- **Interfaces:** exposes `routeStatic` and the app's module contract (`api.mjs`, `store.mjs`, `app.mjs` props and its `launched` hook, `screens.mjs`, the CSS tokens, the `sw.js` placeholders); uses the routes and JSON of Stories 6 to 8
- **Verify:** `CGO_ENABLED=0 go test ./internal/web/... ./tools/... && bash -c 'for f in internal/web/static/js/*.mjs internal/web/static/lib/*.mjs; do node --check "$f" || exit 1; done; node --check internal/web/static/sw.js'`
- **Review:** deep

`routeStatic` serves the embedded files, writing the build ID and the shell list into `sw.js` and the build ID into `index.html`, and fails closed (a 500 on `/`, a logged error) when the embedded app cannot be served. The shell holds the vendored Preact + htm module (pinned by hash), the stylesheet's tokens in both themes, the API and stream client, the snapshot cache, the router with its stale bar and toasts, the Add to Home Screen steps and the pairing screen. The stream client drops a stream silent for 45 s and, when the page becomes visible again, drops a live stream and opens a fresh one, quietly: the spike saw a page on iOS sit on a stream that delivered nothing. `app.mjs` calls an empty `launched` hook once per launch, after the first snapshot, which Story 12 fills with the push check at the points the names contract pins. `tools/webicons` draws the three icons, which are committed, and is named in `tools/CLAUDE.md`'s list of tools. The Add to Home Screen steps carry a "Continue in the browser anyway" link, an addition to the spec's Safari rule (which shows the steps instead of pairing): the user may pair in the browser, and the choice is remembered. No code here holds a landmine marker. The mockups (2c, pairing) could not be read by the drafter, so the pairing and install screens follow the spec's "The app" and "Pairing and auth"; the owner may restyle them.

Decisions private to this story, which Story 10 and 12 rely on only as written below: `call(method, path, body)` and `act` use `path` exactly as given and prepend nothing (a caller passes the whole path, such as `/api/v1/sessions/12/pause`); `pref(key, value)` stores JSON, so strings, numbers and booleans round-trip, a read of a missing or unreadable key returns `null`, and `pref(key, null)` removes the key; `loadSnapshot`, `saveSnapshot` and `clearSnapshot` return promises that never reject; `toast(text, isError, sticky)` takes an optional third argument, which `act` and the stream set for a notice, and an error toast or a sticky one stays until tapped while any other fades after 4 s; `parseRoute` gives `{name: "fleet"}`, `{name: "session", id: <number>}`, `{name: "new"}`, `{name: "settings"}` or `{name: "pair"}`; `connect` also calls an optional `handlers.unauthorized()` on a 401 (and then stops reconnecting); the chrome classes of `app.css` are `.shell`, `.stale-bar`, `.toasts`, `.toast`, `.splash`, `.pair`, `button`, `button.primary`, `button.danger`, `button.link` and `input.text`.

```go
package web

import (
	"fmt"
	"image/color"
	"image/png"
	"os"
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

func TestIcons_AreOpaqueDrawingsOfTheirSize(t *testing.T) {
	for _, size := range []int{180, 192, 512} {
		name := fmt.Sprintf("static/icons/icon-%d.png", size)
		f, err := os.Open(name)
		require.NoError(t, err, name)
		img, err := png.Decode(f)
		require.NoError(t, f.Close())
		require.NoError(t, err, name)

		b := img.Bounds()
		require.Equal(t, size, b.Dx(), name)
		require.Equal(t, size, b.Dy(), name)

		for _, p := range [][2]int{{0, 0}, {size - 1, 0}, {0, size - 1}, {size - 1, size - 1}} {
			_, _, _, a := img.At(p[0], p[1]).RGBA()
			require.Equal(t, uint32(0xffff), a, "%s corner %v is not opaque", name, p)
		}

		colours := map[color.Color]bool{}
		for y := b.Min.Y; y < b.Max.Y; y++ {
			for x := b.Min.X; x < b.Max.X; x++ {
				colours[img.At(x, y)] = true
			}
		}
		require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(colours), 3, "%s is a blank square", name)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `tools/webicons/main.go`:

```go
// Command webicons draws the web app's icons into internal/web/static/icons.
//
// Run it from the repo root: go run ./tools/webicons. The icons are
// committed, so a build needs no step; run it again only to change the
// drawing. The drawing is a small weave in loom's default theme (afterglow):
// the accent warp and the workspace-colour weft on the ground colour, inside
// the middle three fifths of the square so a mask can round it.
package main

import (
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"image"
	"image/color"
	"image/draw"
	"image/png"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
)

// sizes are the icon edges the manifest and apple-touch-icon use.
var sizes = []int{180, 192, 512}

// threads is the number of warp threads and of weft threads.
const threads = 5

var (
	ground = color.RGBA{R: 0x2e, G: 0x2e, B: 0x2e, A: 0xff}
	warp   = color.RGBA{R: 0x6c, G: 0x99, B: 0xbb, A: 0xff}
	weft   = color.RGBA{R: 0xa1, G: 0x61, B: 0x7a, A: 0xff}
)

// drawIcon draws one square icon: horizontal threads, vertical threads, then
// the weft repainted over the warp at every other crossing.
func drawIcon(size int) *image.RGBA {
	img := image.NewRGBA(image.Rect(0, 0, size, size))
	fill := func(c color.RGBA, x0, y0, x1, y1 int) {
		draw.Draw(img, image.Rect(x0, y0, x1, y1), image.NewUniform(c), image.Point{}, draw.Src)
	}
	fill(ground, 0, 0, size, size)

	margin := size / 5
	pitch := (size - 2*margin) / threads
	thick := pitch * 3 / 5
	if thick < 1 {
		thick = 1
	}
	span := pitch * threads
	origin := (size - span) / 2
	at := func(i int) int { return origin + i*pitch + (pitch-thick)/2 }

	for j := 0; j < threads; j++ {
		fill(weft, origin, at(j), origin+span, at(j)+thick)
	}
	for i := 0; i < threads; i++ {
		fill(warp, at(i), origin, at(i)+thick, origin+span)
	}
	for i := 0; i < threads; i++ {
		for j := 0; j < threads; j++ {
			if (i+j)%2 == 1 {
				fill(weft, at(i), at(j), at(i)+thick, at(j)+thick)
			}
		}
	}
	return img
}

// writeIcon writes icon-<size>.png into dir.
func writeIcon(dir string, size int) error {
	name := filepath.Join(dir, fmt.Sprintf("icon-%d.png", size))
	f, err := os.Create(name)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("create %s: %w", name, err)
	}
	enc := png.Encoder{CompressionLevel: png.BestCompression}
	if err := enc.Encode(f, drawIcon(size)); err != nil {
		_ = f.Close()
		return fmt.Errorf("encode %s: %w", name, err)
	}
	if err := f.Close(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("close %s: %w", name, err)
	}
	return nil
}

func main() {
	out := flag.String("out", filepath.Join("internal", "web", "static", "icons"), "directory to write the icons into")
	flag.Parse()

	if err := os.MkdirAll(*out, 0o755); err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "webicons:", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
	for _, size := range sizes {
		if err := writeIcon(*out, size); err != nil {
			fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "webicons:", err)
			os.Exit(1)
		}
	}
}
```

Then run it from the repo root to write the three PNGs: `CGO_ENABLED=0 go run ./tools/webicons`

```go
// The vendored module is htm 3.1.1's preact/standalone.module.js, byte for
// byte after a one-line header comment.
const (
	preactHTMURL    = "https://unpkg.com/htm@3.1.1/preact/standalone.module.js"
	preactHTMSize   = 13194
	preactHTMSHA256 = "72284e8e9079c87817145df1110f74e8a2aa040b2fc384922e18dfcb46fc1fd7"
)

func TestPreactHTM_IsThePinnedFile(t *testing.T) {
	raw, err := os.ReadFile("static/lib/preact-htm.mjs")
	require.NoError(t, err)

	header, body, ok := bytes.Cut(raw, []byte("\n"))
	require.True(t, ok, "the file is one line")
	require.True(t, bytes.HasPrefix(header, []byte("//")), "the first line is a comment")
	for _, want := range []string{"htm 3.1.1", preactHTMURL, preactHTMSHA256, "NOTICE.md"} {
		require.Contains(t, string(header), want)
	}

	require.Len(t, body, preactHTMSize)
	sum := sha256.Sum256(body)
	require.Equal(t, preactHTMSHA256, hex.EncodeToString(sum[:]))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Fetch the pinned file outside the worktree, check it, and write the module with its header line (run from the repo root):

```bash
dl=$(mktemp -d)
curl -sSfL -o "$dl/standalone.module.js" https://unpkg.com/htm@3.1.1/preact/standalone.module.js
wc -c < "$dl/standalone.module.js"
sha256sum "$dl/standalone.module.js"
mkdir -p internal/web/static/lib
{ printf '%s\n' '// htm 3.1.1, preact/standalone.module.js, from https://unpkg.com/htm@3.1.1/preact/standalone.module.js. The lines below are that file unchanged: 13194 bytes, SHA-256 72284e8e9079c87817145df1110f74e8a2aa040b2fc384922e18dfcb46fc1fd7. Apache-2.0 (htm) and MIT (Preact), see NOTICE.md.'; cat "$dl/standalone.module.js"; } > internal/web/static/lib/preact-htm.mjs
rm -r "$dl"
```

`wc -c` must print `13194` and `sha256sum` must print `72284e8e9079c87817145df1110f74e8a2aa040b2fc384922e18dfcb46fc1fd7`; if either differs, stop and report it: the pin is the spec's. The module exports `h`, `html`, `render`, `Component`, `createContext`, `useState`, `useReducer`, `useEffect`, `useLayoutEffect`, `useRef`, `useImperativeHandle`, `useMemo`, `useCallback`, `useContext`, `useDebugValue` and `useErrorBoundary` (no `Fragment`).

```go
func staticMux(t *testing.T, build string) *http.ServeMux {
	t.Helper()
	m := http.NewServeMux()
	(&Layer{build: build}).routeStatic(m)
	return m
}

func staticDo(h http.Handler, method, target string) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	h.ServeHTTP(rec, httptest.NewRequest(method, target, nil))
	return rec
}

// staticAssets is the embedded app as routeStatic serves it.
func staticAssets(t *testing.T) map[string]staticAsset {
	t.Helper()
	sub, err := fs.Sub(staticFiles, "static")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assets, err := buildStaticAssets(sub, "b-test")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return assets
}

func TestStatic_ServesEachFileWithItsType(t *testing.T) {
	m := staticMux(t, "b-1")
	for _, tc := range []struct{ url, ctype string }{
		{"/", "text/html; charset=utf-8"},
		{"/index.html", "text/html; charset=utf-8"},
		{"/app.css", "text/css; charset=utf-8"},
		{"/sw.js", "text/javascript; charset=utf-8"},
		{"/manifest.webmanifest", "application/manifest+json"},
		{"/lib/preact-htm.mjs", "text/javascript; charset=utf-8"},
		{"/icons/icon-512.png", "image/png"},
	} {
		rec := staticDo(m, http.MethodGet, tc.url)
		require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, tc.url)
		require.Equal(t, tc.ctype, rec.Header().Get("Content-Type"), tc.url)
		require.Equal(t, "no-cache", rec.Header().Get("Cache-Control"), tc.url)
		require.NotEmpty(t, rec.Body.Bytes(), tc.url)
	}
}

func TestStatic_RefusesWhatIsNotAFile(t *testing.T) {
	m := staticMux(t, "b-1")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusMethodNotAllowed, staticDo(m, http.MethodPost, "/").Code)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusMethodNotAllowed, staticDo(m, http.MethodPut, "/app.css").Code)
	for _, target := range []string{"/nope", "/static/index.html", "/js/", "/api/v1/anything"} {
		require.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, staticDo(m, http.MethodGet, target).Code, target)
	}
	// An unclean path is redirected by the mux, never served.
	require.NotEqual(t, http.StatusOK, staticDo(m, http.MethodGet, "/../static.go").Code)
}

func TestStatic_IndexCarriesTheBuild(t *testing.T) {
	build := `b-"1"<x>&'`
	m := staticMux(t, build)
	index := staticDo(m, http.MethodGet, "/").Body.String()

	require.NotContains(t, index, "__LOOM_")
	meta := regexp.MustCompile(`<meta name="loom-build" content="([^"]*)">`).FindStringSubmatch(index)
	require.NotNil(t, meta, "index.html lost its loom-build meta")
	require.Equal(t, build, html.UnescapeString(meta[1]))
	require.Equal(t, index, staticDo(m, http.MethodGet, "/index.html").Body.String())
}

func TestStatic_ServiceWorkerCarriesTheBuildAndTheShell(t *testing.T) {
	build := `b-"1"<x>&`
	sw := staticDo(staticMux(t, build), http.MethodGet, "/sw.js").Body.String()
	require.NotContains(t, sw, "__LOOM_")

	buildLine := regexp.MustCompile(`(?m)^const BUILD = (.*);$`).FindStringSubmatch(sw)
	require.NotNil(t, buildLine, "sw.js lost its BUILD line")
	var gotBuild string
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal([]byte(buildLine[1]), &gotBuild))
	require.Equal(t, build, gotBuild)

	shellLine := regexp.MustCompile(`(?m)^const SHELL = (.*);$`).FindStringSubmatch(sw)
	require.NotNil(t, shellLine, "sw.js lost its SHELL line")
	var shell []string
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal([]byte(shellLine[1]), &shell))

	want := []string{"/"}
	require.NoError(t, fs.WalkDir(staticFiles, "static", func(name string, d fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
		if err == nil && !d.IsDir() {
			want = append(want, strings.TrimPrefix(name, "static"))
		}
		return err
	}))
	require.ElementsMatch(t, want, shell)
}

func TestStatic_BuildRefusesAnAppThatCannotBeServed(t *testing.T) {
	good := func() fstest.MapFS {
		return fstest.MapFS{
			"index.html": {Data: []byte(`<meta name="loom-build" content="__LOOM_BUILD__">`)},
			"sw.js":      {Data: []byte("const BUILD = \"__LOOM_BUILD__\";\nconst SHELL = [\"__LOOM_SHELL__\"];\n")},
		}
	}
	assets, err := buildStaticAssets(good(), "b-7")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Contains(t, string(assets["/index.html"].body), `content="b-7"`)
	require.Contains(t, string(assets["/sw.js"].body), `const BUILD = "b-7";`)
	require.Contains(t, string(assets["/sw.js"].body), `const SHELL = ["/","/index.html","/sw.js"];`)

	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name string
		edit func(fstest.MapFS)
		want string
	}{
		{"no index", func(f fstest.MapFS) { delete(f, "index.html") }, "no /index.html"},
		{"index without its build", func(f fstest.MapFS) { f["index.html"] = &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("<html>")} }, "/index.html has no"},
		{"no service worker", func(f fstest.MapFS) { delete(f, "sw.js") }, "no /sw.js"},
		{"service worker without BUILD", func(f fstest.MapFS) {
			f["sw.js"] = &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("const SHELL = [\"__LOOM_SHELL__\"];\n")}
		}, "/sw.js has no"},
		{"service worker without SHELL", func(f fstest.MapFS) {
			f["sw.js"] = &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("const BUILD = \"__LOOM_BUILD__\";\n")}
		}, "/sw.js has no"},
		{"a file of an unknown type", func(f fstest.MapFS) { f["notes.txt"] = &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("x")} }, "no content type"},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			f := good()
			tc.edit(f)
			_, err := buildStaticAssets(f, "b-7")
			require.ErrorContains(t, err, tc.want)
		})
	}
}

	require.Positive(t, dark, "app.css has no dark theme block")

	for _, token := range []string{"bg", "panel", "fg", "dim", "rule", "accent", "attention", "ok", "error", "workspace"} {

		require.Contains(t, darkBlock, "--"+token+":", "dark theme lacks --"+token)

// staticInlineMarkup lists what the Content-Security-Policy would block in
// markup: an inline script, a style attribute, an event-handler attribute.
func staticInlineMarkup(markup string) []string {
	var found []string
	for _, tag := range regexp.MustCompile(`(?is)<script\b[^>]*>`).FindAllString(markup, -1) {
		if !regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)\ssrc=`).MatchString(tag) {
			found = append(found, "inline script: "+tag)
		}
	}
	for _, attr := range regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)\s(style|on[a-z]+)\s*=`).FindAllString(markup, -1) {
		found = append(found, "attribute: "+strings.TrimSpace(attr))
	}
	return found
}

func TestStatic_InlineMarkupDetector(t *testing.T) {
	require.Empty(t, staticInlineMarkup(`<script type="module" src="/js/app.mjs"></script><p class="a">x</p>`))
	require.Len(t, staticInlineMarkup(`<script>alert(1)</script>`), 1)
	require.Len(t, staticInlineMarkup(`<p style="color: red">x</p>`), 1)
	require.Len(t, staticInlineMarkup(`<a onclick="go()">x</a>`), 1)
}

func TestStatic_IndexHasNoInlineScriptOrStyle(t *testing.T) {
	require.Empty(t, staticInlineMarkup(string(staticAssets(t)["/index.html"].body)))
}

// The mux needs a started layer to run, so the wiring is pinned by its text.
func TestMux_RegistersTheStaticRoutes(t *testing.T) {
	src, err := os.ReadFile("web.go")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Regexp(t, `(?m)^\tl\.routeStatic\(m\)\n\treturn m$`, string(src))
}
```

Create `internal/web/static.go`:

```go
package web

import (
	"bytes"
	"embed"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"html"
	"io/fs"
	"net/http"
	"path"
	"strconv"

	"github.com/aidan-bailey/loom/log"
)

// staticFiles is the app: the shell, its modules, the stylesheet, the service
// worker, the manifest and the icons.
//
//go:embed static
var staticFiles embed.FS

const (
	staticBuildMark = "__LOOM_BUILD__"
	staticShellMark = "__LOOM_SHELL__"
)

// staticContentTypes is every extension the app's files may have. A file of
// another extension fails the build of the assets, so a new kind of file is a
// decision here, never a guessed type.
var staticContentTypes = map[string]string{
	".html":        "text/html; charset=utf-8",
	".css":         "text/css; charset=utf-8",
	".js":          "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
	".mjs":         "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
	".webmanifest": "application/manifest+json",
	".png":         "image/png",
}

// staticAsset is one served file.
type staticAsset struct {
	body  []byte
	ctype string
}

func (a staticAsset) serve(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
	h := w.Header()
	h.Set("Content-Type", a.ctype)
	h.Set("Cache-Control", "no-cache")
	h.Set("Content-Length", strconv.Itoa(len(a.body)))
	if _, err := w.Write(a.body); err != nil {
		log.For("web").Debug("the app's file was not delivered", "err", err)
	}
}

// buildStaticAssets reads every file of fsys into a map keyed by URL path. It
// fills the build ID into index.html and sw.js and the list of every file's
// URL (and "/") into sw.js, and serves index.html at "/" too. It fails closed:
// a missing placeholder, a missing index.html or sw.js, or a file of an
// unknown type is an error, so the worker's precache list cannot drift from
// the embedded files.
func buildStaticAssets(fsys fs.FS, build string) (map[string]staticAsset, error) {
	assets := make(map[string]staticAsset)
	var urls []string
	err := fs.WalkDir(fsys, ".", func(name string, d fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if d.IsDir() {
			return nil
		}
		ctype, ok := staticContentTypes[path.Ext(name)]
		if !ok {
			return fmt.Errorf("%s: no content type for %q", name, path.Ext(name))
		}
		body, err := fs.ReadFile(fsys, name)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		urls = append(urls, "/"+name)
		assets["/"+name] = staticAsset{body: body, ctype: ctype}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("read the app's files: %w", err)
	}

	shell, err := json.Marshal(append([]string{"/"}, urls...))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("encode the shell list: %w", err)
	}
	buildJSON, err := json.Marshal(build)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("encode the build: %w", err)
	}
	if err := fillStatic(assets, "/index.html", staticBuildMark, html.EscapeString(build)); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := fillStatic(assets, "/sw.js", `"`+staticBuildMark+`"`, string(buildJSON)); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := fillStatic(assets, "/sw.js", `["`+staticShellMark+`"]`, string(shell)); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	assets["/"] = assets["/index.html"]
	return assets, nil
}

// fillStatic replaces every old in the asset at url with repl, and errors when
// the asset or the placeholder is not there.
func fillStatic(assets map[string]staticAsset, url, old, repl string) error {
	a, ok := assets[url]
	if !ok {
		return errors.New("the app has no " + url)
	}
	if !bytes.Contains(a.body, []byte(old)) {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s has no %s to fill", url, old)
	}
	a.body = bytes.ReplaceAll(a.body, []byte(old), []byte(repl))
	assets[url] = a
	return nil
}

// routeStatic serves the app shell and its files: public, with no data in
// them. When the embedded app cannot be served it logs why and answers 500 on
// "/", so a broken build is loud rather than a blank page.
func (l *Layer) routeStatic(m *http.ServeMux) {
	sub, err := fs.Sub(staticFiles, "static")
	var assets map[string]staticAsset
	if err == nil {
		assets, err = buildStaticAssets(sub, l.build)
	}
	if err != nil {
		log.For("web").Error("the app's files cannot be served", "err", err)
		m.HandleFunc("GET /{$}", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
			http.Error(w, "the app's files are missing from this build", http.StatusInternalServerError)
		})
		return
	}
	for url, a := range assets {
		pattern := "GET " + url
		if url == "/" {
			pattern = "GET /{$}"
		}
		m.HandleFunc(pattern, a.serve)
	}
}
```

Create `internal/web/static/sw.js` (Story 12 appends its handlers to the end of this file and changes nothing above it):

```js
// Loom's service worker. It caches the app shell and never the API. The
// server writes BUILD and SHELL when it serves this file: BUILD is the
// daemon's build ID, SHELL the URL of every embedded file plus "/".
const BUILD = "__LOOM_BUILD__";
const SHELL = ["__LOOM_SHELL__"];
const PREFIX = "loom-shell-";
const CACHE = PREFIX + BUILD;

self.addEventListener("install", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches
      .open(CACHE)
      .then((cache) => cache.addAll(SHELL.map((url) => new Request(url, { cache: "reload" }))))
      .then(() => self.skipWaiting()),
  );
});

self.addEventListener("activate", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches
      .keys()
      .then((keys) =>
        Promise.all(keys.filter((key) => key.startsWith(PREFIX) && key !== CACHE).map((key) => caches.delete(key))),
      )
      .then(() => self.clients.claim()),
  );
});

self.addEventListener("fetch", (event) => {
  const req = event.request;
  if (req.method !== "GET") return;
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  if (url.origin !== self.location.origin || url.pathname.startsWith("/api/")) return;
  const key = req.mode === "navigate" ? "/" : url.pathname;
  event.respondWith(caches.open(CACHE).then((cache) => cache.match(key).then((hit) => hit || fetch(req))));
});
```

```css
/* Loom's app. The colour tokens come from loom's theme (ui/theme.go): the
   dark block is afterglow, loom's default; the light block is the legacy
   theme's light side, with the amber and the dim text darkened to read on

:root {

  min-height: 100vh;
  min-height: 100dvh;
  padding: env(safe-area-inset-top) env(safe-area-inset-right) env(safe-area-inset-bottom) env(safe-area-inset-left);
}

.shell > main {
  flex: 1;
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 40rem;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 1rem;
}

h1,
h2,
h3,
p {
  margin: 0 0 0.75rem;
}

code {
  font: 0.9em ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace;
  background: var(--panel);
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 0.1em 0.35em;
}

button {
  font: inherit;
  color: var(--fg);
  background: var(--panel);
  border: 1px solid var(--rule);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  min-height: 44px;
  padding: 0.6rem 1rem;
}

button:disabled {
  opacity: 0.45;
}

button.primary {
  background: var(--accent);
  border-color: var(--accent);
  color: var(--bg);
  font-weight: 600;
}

button.danger {
  color: var(--error);
  border-color: var(--error);
}

button.link {
  background: none;
  border: none;
  color: var(--dim);
  text-decoration: underline;
}

input.text {
  font: inherit;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 44px;
  padding: 0.6rem 0.8rem;
  color: var(--fg);
  background: var(--panel);
  border: 1px solid var(--rule);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
}

input.text:focus {
  outline: 2px solid var(--accent);
  outline-offset: 1px;
}

.splash {
  padding: 3rem 0;
  text-align: center;
  color: var(--dim);
}

.stale-bar {
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 10;
  padding: 0.4rem 1rem;
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 0.85rem;
  color: var(--dim);
  background: var(--panel);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rule);
}

.toasts {
  position: fixed;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  bottom: calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 0.75rem);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 0 1rem;
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 100;
}

.toast {
  max-width: 32rem;
  padding: 0.65rem 1rem;
  color: var(--fg);
  background: var(--panel);
  border: 1px solid var(--rule);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  box-shadow: 0 2px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
  pointer-events: auto;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.toast.error {
  border-color: var(--error);
}

.pair {
  padding-top: 2rem;
}

.pair .wordmark {
  font-size: 2rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
}

.pair .lede {
  color: var(--dim);
}

.pair .steps {
  padding-left: 1.25rem;
  margin: 0 0 1.25rem;
}

.pair .steps li {
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.pair form {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.75rem;
}

.pair input.code {
  font: 1.6rem ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace;
  letter-spacing: 0.15em;
  text-align: center;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.pair .form-error {
  margin-top: 0.75rem;
  color: var(--error);
}

.pair .hint {
  color: var(--dim);
  font-size: 0.9rem;
}
```

These tests cover every module in the app, including the ones Stories 10 and 12 add: a reference to a file that is not embedded, an import of a name its module does not export (`Fragment`, which the vendored module lacks, is the likely one), or a style attribute fails here.

```go
var (
	staticAttrRef   = regexp.MustCompile(`(?:src|href)="(/[^"]*)"`)
	staticImportRef = regexp.MustCompile(`(?:from|import)\s*\(?\s*"(\.{1,2}/[^"]+)"`)
	staticNamedRef  = regexp.MustCompile(`import\s*\{([^}]*)\}\s*from\s*"(\.{1,2}/[^"]+)"`)
	staticExportFn  = regexp.MustCompile(`export\s+(?:async\s+)?(?:function\*?|const|let|var|class)\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)`)
	staticExportSet = regexp.MustCompile(`export\s*\{([^}]*)\}`)
)

// staticModules lists the URLs of the app's modules, the vendored one included.
func staticModules(assets map[string]staticAsset) []string {
	var urls []string
	for url := range assets {
		if strings.HasSuffix(url, ".mjs") {
			urls = append(urls, url)
		}
	}
	return urls
}

// staticExports lists the names a module exports.
func staticExports(body []byte) map[string]bool {
	names := map[string]bool{}
	for _, m := range staticExportFn.FindAllSubmatch(body, -1) {
		names[string(m[1])] = true
	}
	for _, m := range staticExportSet.FindAllSubmatch(body, -1) {
		for _, item := range strings.Split(string(m[1]), ",") {
			parts := strings.Fields(item)
			if len(parts) > 0 {
				names[parts[len(parts)-1]] = true
			}
		}
	}
	return names
}

func TestStatic_EveryReferenceResolves(t *testing.T) {
	assets := staticAssets(t)

	for _, ref := range staticAttrRef.FindAllStringSubmatch(string(assets["/index.html"].body), -1) {
		require.Contains(t, assets, ref[1], "index.html references a file that is not embedded")
	}
	for _, url := range staticModules(assets) {
		for _, ref := range staticImportRef.FindAllStringSubmatch(string(assets[url].body), -1) {
			target := path.Join(path.Dir(url), ref[1])
			require.Contains(t, assets, target, "%s imports %s, which is not embedded", url, ref[1])
		}
	}
	require.Contains(t, assets, "/js/app.mjs")
}

func TestStatic_NamedImportsAreExported(t *testing.T) {
	assets := staticAssets(t)
	vendored := staticExports(assets["/lib/preact-htm.mjs"].body)
	require.True(t, vendored["useState"], "the export reader misses the vendored module's list")
	require.False(t, vendored["Fragment"])

	for _, url := range staticModules(assets) {
		for _, imp := range staticNamedRef.FindAllStringSubmatch(string(assets[url].body), -1) {
			target := path.Join(path.Dir(url), imp[2])
			exports := staticExports(assets[target].body)
			for _, item := range strings.Split(imp[1], ",") {
				parts := strings.Fields(item)
				if len(parts) == 0 {
					continue
				}
				require.True(t, exports[parts[0]], "%s imports %s, which %s does not export", url, parts[0], target)
			}
		}
	}
}

func TestStatic_ModulesSetNoStyleAttribute(t *testing.T) {
	assets := staticAssets(t)
	for _, url := range staticModules(assets) {
		if url == "/lib/preact-htm.mjs" {
			continue
		}
		require.Empty(t, regexp.MustCompile(`\sstyle=`).FindAllString(string(assets[url].body), -1),
			"%s sets a style attribute: the app uses classes", url)
	}
}
```
