Create `internal/web/static/js/store.mjs`:

```js
// The phone's own storage: the last snapshot in IndexedDB, small preferences
// in localStorage. Every access can fail (private mode, a full disk, a
// blocked database), and none may break the app, so each is guarded and a
// failure reads as "nothing stored".
const DB_NAME = "loom";
const STORE = "snapshot";
const KEY = "latest";

function openDB() {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (!("indexedDB" in window)) {
      reject(new Error("no indexedDB"));
      return;
    }
    const req = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, 1);
    req.onupgradeneeded = () => {
      req.result.createObjectStore(STORE);
    };
    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);
    req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
    req.onblocked = () => reject(new Error("indexedDB is blocked"));
  });
}

// withStore runs one request in a transaction and resolves its result when
// the transaction completes.
async function withStore(mode, run) {
  const db = await openDB();
  try {
    return await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const tx = db.transaction(STORE, mode);
      const request = run(tx.objectStore(STORE));
      tx.oncomplete = () => resolve(request.result);
      tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
      tx.onabort = () => reject(tx.error);
    });
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

// loadSnapshot, saveSnapshot and clearSnapshot never reject.
// loadSnapshot resolves the last saved snapshot, or null.
export async function loadSnapshot() {
  try {
    const snap = await withStore("readonly", (store) => store.get(KEY));
    return snap === undefined ? null : snap;
  } catch (err) {
    return null;
  }
}

// saveSnapshot stores the snapshot and resolves whether it worked.
export async function saveSnapshot(snap) {
  try {
    await withStore("readwrite", (store) => store.put(snap, KEY));
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    return false;
  }
}

// clearSnapshot forgets the stored snapshot (on a 401 and on unpairing).
export async function clearSnapshot() {
  try {
    await withStore("readwrite", (store) => store.delete(KEY));
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    return false;
  }
}

// pref reads (value undefined) or writes (value given) the localStorage key
// "loom.<key>". Values are stored as JSON, so strings, numbers and booleans
// round-trip; a null value removes the key. A read of a key that is not set
// or cannot be read, and any failure, gives null.
export function pref(key, value) {
  const name = "loom." + key;
  try {
    if (value === undefined) {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(name);
      return raw === null ? null : JSON.parse(raw);
    }
    if (value === null) {
      localStorage.removeItem(name);
      return null;
    }
    localStorage.setItem(name, JSON.stringify(value));
    return value;
  } catch (err) {
    return null;
  }
}
```

Create `internal/web/static/js/api.mjs`:

```js
// The API and stream client. Nothing here throws on an HTTP status.

// call sends one request to path, which is the whole path (such as
// "/api/v1/sessions/12/pause"; nothing is prepended), and resolves
// {status, data}: data is the parsed JSON body, or null when there is none (a
// 204). A network failure resolves status 0 with {error: "Can't reach loom."}.
export async function call(method, path, body) {
  const init = { method, credentials: "same-origin", headers: {} };
  if (body !== undefined) {
    init.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
    init.body = JSON.stringify(body);
  }
  let res;
  try {
    res = await fetch(path, init);
  } catch (err) {
    return { status: 0, data: { error: "Can't reach loom." } };
  }
  let data = null;
  if (res.status !== 204) {
    try {
      data = await res.json();
    } catch (err) {
      data = null;
    }
  }
  return { status: res.status, data };
}

const FIRST_DELAY = 1000;
const MAX_DELAY = 30000;
// The server pings every 20 s; a stream that is silent for more than twice
// that is dead, whatever the phone's network layer believes.
const SILENCE = 45000;

// connect opens the event stream and calls handlers[event](data) for each
// event, handlers.open() once a response arrives and handlers.error() when the
// stream ends or fails. It reconnects with a backoff from 1 s to 30 s, and at
// once when the phone comes online. When the page becomes visible again it
// drops a live stream and opens a fresh one without handlers.error(): a page
// iOS resumes can sit on a stream that delivers nothing, and the fresh
// snapshot replaces the view. On a 401 it calls handlers.unauthorized() and
// stops. It returns {close()}.
export function connect(handlers) {
  let closed = false;
  let running = false;
  let live = false;
  let refreshing = false;
  let delay = FIRST_DELAY;
  let controller = null;
  let timer = 0;
  let watchdog = 0;

  function notify(name, arg) {
    const fn = handlers[name];
    if (typeof fn !== "function") return;
    try {
      fn(arg);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loom: handler " + name + " failed", err);
    }
  }

  function dispatch(block) {
    let event = "message";
    const data = [];
    for (const line of block.split("\n")) {
      if (line === "" || line.startsWith(":")) continue;
      const colon = line.indexOf(":");
      const field = colon < 0 ? line : line.slice(0, colon);
      let value = colon < 0 ? "" : line.slice(colon + 1);
      if (value.startsWith(" ")) value = value.slice(1);
      if (field === "event") event = value;
      else if (field === "data") data.push(value);
    }
    if (data.length === 0) return;
    if (event === "open" || event === "error" || event === "unauthorized") return;
    let parsed;
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(data.join("\n"));
    } catch (err) {
      return;
    }
    notify(event, parsed);
  }

  async function session() {
    controller = new AbortController();
    const mine = controller;
    const arm = () => {
      clearTimeout(watchdog);
      watchdog = setTimeout(() => mine.abort(), SILENCE);
    };
    try {
      arm();
      const res = await fetch("/api/v1/stream", {
        credentials: "same-origin",
        cache: "no-store",
        headers: { Accept: "text/event-stream" },
        signal: mine.signal,
      });
      if (res.status === 401) {
        closed = true;
        notify("unauthorized");
        return;
      }
      if (!res.ok || !res.body) throw new Error("stream answered " + res.status);
      live = true;
      delay = FIRST_DELAY;
      notify("open");
      const reader = res.body.getReader();
      const decoder = new TextDecoder();
      let buf = "";
      for (;;) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        arm();
        buf += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true }).replace(/\r/g, "");
        let end;
        while ((end = buf.indexOf("\n\n")) >= 0) {
          dispatch(buf.slice(0, end));
          buf = buf.slice(end + 2);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      // fall through: the stream is down, the caller reconnects
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(watchdog);
      live = false;
    }
  }

  async function run() {
    if (closed || running) return;
    running = true;
    try {
      await session();
    } finally {
      running = false;
    }
    if (closed) return;
    if (refreshing) {
      // onVisible dropped it on purpose: open the next one now, quietly.
      refreshing = false;
      delay = FIRST_DELAY;
      run();
      return;
    }
    notify("error");
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(run, delay);
    delay = Math.min(delay * 2, MAX_DELAY);
  }

  function wake() {
    if (closed || running || live) return;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    delay = FIRST_DELAY;
    run();
  }

  // onVisible refreshes a live stream, which may be dead without a sign the
  // watchdog would see for up to SILENCE, and wakes one that is down.
  function onVisible() {
    if (document.visibilityState !== "visible") return;
    if (live && controller) {
      refreshing = true;
      controller.abort();
      return;
    }
    wake();
  }

  document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisible);
  window.addEventListener("online", wake);
  run();

  return {
    close() {
      closed = true;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      clearTimeout(watchdog);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisible);
      window.removeEventListener("online", wake);
      if (controller) controller.abort();
    },
  };
}
```

Create `internal/web/static/js/pair.mjs`:

```js
// The two screens before the fleet: the Add to Home Screen steps, shown in
// Safari on an iPhone or iPad (the installed app keeps its own cookies, so
// pairing in Safari would pair the wrong place), and pairing itself.
import { html, useState } from "../lib/preact-htm.mjs";
import { call } from "./api.mjs";
import { pref } from "./store.mjs";

const NOT_IN_ALPHABET = /[^23456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ]/g;
const CODE_LEN = 8;

// needsInstall reports whether to show the Add to Home Screen steps: on an
// iPhone or iPad, outside the installed app, unless the user chose the
// browser.
export function needsInstall() {
  const ua = navigator.userAgent || "";
  const ios = /iPhone|iPad|iPod/.test(ua) || (navigator.platform === "MacIntel" && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1);
  const standalone =
    navigator.standalone === true || (window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia("(display-mode: standalone)").matches);
  return ios && !standalone && pref("browser") !== true;
}

// formatCode upper-cases what was typed, drops what the alphabet lacks and
// puts the dash after four characters: K7QM-2XPA.
function formatCode(raw) {
  const code = raw.toUpperCase().replace(NOT_IN_ALPHABET, "").slice(0, CODE_LEN);
  return code.length > 4 ? code.slice(0, 4) + "-" + code.slice(4) : code;
}

// pairError words the answer of POST /api/v1/pair: the server's own text for
// 400 (a wrong, used or expired code), 429 (locked), 409 (the name is taken or
// 16 devices are paired) and 503 (the web is off), and for a 429 also says
// what to do.
function pairError(status, data) {
  if (status === 0) return "Can't reach loom. Check the connection and try again.";
  const text = data && data.error ? data.error : "That didn't work.";
  if (status === 429) return text + " Run loom web pair on your computer again for a new code.";
  return text;
}

export function AddToHome({ onContinue }) {
  return html`
    <div class="shell">
      <main>
        <div class="pair">
          <h1 class="wordmark">Loom</h1>
          <p class="lede">Add Loom to your Home Screen to use it. The installed app keeps its own pairing, separate from this tab.</p>
          <ol class="steps">
            <li>Tap the Share button in Safari's toolbar.</li>
            <li>Scroll down and tap <strong>Add to Home Screen</strong>.</li>
            <li>Tap <strong>Add</strong>, then open Loom from your Home Screen.</li>
          </ol>
          <p class="hint">The app then asks for a pairing code.</p>
          <button type="button" class="link" onClick=${onContinue}>Continue in the browser anyway</button>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  `;
}

export function Pair({ onPaired }) {
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const ready = code.replace("-", "").length === CODE_LEN;

  const submit = async (ev) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    if (!ready || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setMessage("");
    const { status, data } = await call("POST", "/api/v1/pair", { code });
    setBusy(false);
    if (status >= 200 && status < 300 && data && data.device) {
      onPaired(data.device);
      return;
    }
    setMessage(pairError(status, data));
  };

  return html`
    <div class="pair">
      <h1 class="wordmark">Loom</h1>
      <p class="lede">Pair this phone with your loom.</p>
      <ol class="steps">
        <li>On the computer running loom, run <code>loom web pair</code>.</li>
        <li>Enter the code it prints. It works once, for 10 minutes.</li>
      </ol>
      <form onSubmit=${submit}>
        <input
          class="text code"
          type="text"
          inputmode="text"
          autocapitalize="characters"
          autocomplete="off"
          autocorrect="off"
          spellcheck="false"
          autofocus
          placeholder="K7QM-2XPA"
          aria-label="Pairing code"
          value=${code}
          onInput=${(ev) => setCode(formatCode(ev.target.value))}
        />
        <button type="submit" class="primary" disabled=${!ready || busy}>${busy ? "Pairing…" : "Pair"}</button>
      </form>
      ${message ? html`<p class="form-error" role="alert">${message}</p>` : null}
    </div>
  `;
}
```

Create `internal/web/static/js/app.mjs`:

```js
// The app: routes, the live snapshot, the stale bar and the toasts. The
// screens are in screens.mjs and pairing in pair.mjs; this file owns the data
// they draw, as the props {snap, stale, route, go, act, toast}.
import { html, render, useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from "../lib/preact-htm.mjs";
import { call, connect } from "./api.mjs";
import { loadSnapshot, saveSnapshot, clearSnapshot, pref } from "./store.mjs";
import { screens } from "./screens.mjs";
import { Pair, AddToHome, needsInstall } from "./pair.mjs";

const buildMeta = document.querySelector('meta[name="loom-build"]');
const shellBuild = buildMeta ? buildMeta.content : "";
const recentNotices = 20;

// parseRoute reads the location's hash: "" is the fleet, "#s/<id>" a session,
// "#new", "#settings", "#pair"; anything else is the fleet. The session's id
// is a number, as in the JSON.
export function parseRoute(hash) {
  const h = (hash || "").replace(/^#\/?/, "");
  if (h === "new") return { name: "new" };
  if (h === "settings") return { name: "settings" };
  if (h === "pair") return { name: "pair" };
  const m = h.match(/^s\/(\d+)$/);
  if (m) return { name: "session", id: Number(m[1]) };
  return { name: "fleet" };
}

let reloading = false;
function reload() {
  if (reloading) return;
  reloading = true;
  location.reload();
}

// upgradeShell runs when the daemon's build differs from the shell's: it asks
// the service worker for the new shell, which installs, takes over and
// reloads the page. Once per build, so a shell that cannot update does not
// reload forever.
function upgradeShell(build) {
  try {
    if (sessionStorage.getItem("loom.upgrade") === build) return;
    sessionStorage.setItem("loom.upgrade", build);
  } catch (err) {
    // no sessionStorage: the reload flag above still allows one reload
  }
  if (!("serviceWorker" in navigator)) {
    reload();
    return;
  }
  navigator.serviceWorker
    .getRegistration()
    .then((reg) => {
      if (!reg) {
        reload();
        return;
      }
      reg.update().catch(() => {});
      setTimeout(reload, 5000);
    })
    .catch(reload);
}

if ("serviceWorker" in navigator) {
  const hadController = Boolean(navigator.serviceWorker.controller);
  navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener("controllerchange", () => {
    if (hadController) reload();
  });
  navigator.serviceWorker.register("/sw.js").catch(() => {});
}

// The snapshot is saved at most once a second: sessions events come up to
// four a second.
let saveTimer = 0;
function persist(snap) {
  clearTimeout(saveTimer);
  saveTimer = setTimeout(() => saveSnapshot(snap), 1000);
}

// launched runs once per launch, after the first snapshot, with the app's
// toast: the place for work that needs the device's state once a launch.
function launched(snap, toast) {}
let launchDone = false;

function App() {
  const [snap, setSnap] = useState(null);
  const [stale, setStale] = useState(true);
  const [offline, setOffline] = useState(false);
  const [route, setRoute] = useState(() => parseRoute(location.hash));
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState([]);
  const [install, setInstall] = useState(() => needsInstall());
  const snapRef = useRef(null);
  const staleRef = useRef(true);
  const routeRef = useRef(route);
  const streamRef = useRef(null);
  const nextToast = useRef(1);
  staleRef.current = stale;
  routeRef.current = route;

  // toast shows text. An error toast, and a sticky one (a notice's, since a
  // kill's or resume's stash notice may be the only record of a SHA), stays
  // until tapped; any other fades after 4 s.
  const dismissToast = useCallback((id) => {
    setToasts((all) => all.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const toast = useCallback((text, isError, sticky) => {
    const id = nextToast.current++;
    const stays = Boolean(isError || sticky);
    setToasts((all) => all.concat([{ id, text, error: Boolean(isError) }]));
    if (!stays) setTimeout(() => dismissToast(id), 4000);
  }, []);

  const go = useCallback((hash) => {
    const h = hash.startsWith("#") ? hash : "#" + hash;
    if (location.hash === h || (h === "#" && location.hash === "")) {
      setRoute(parseRoute(h));
      return;
    }
    location.hash = h;
  }, []);

  const stopStream = useCallback(() => {
    if (streamRef.current) {
      streamRef.current.close();
      streamRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  // unpair forgets everything the phone holds and shows pairing; a 401 or the
  // end of the device's own unpairing lands here.
  const unpair = useCallback(() => {
    stopStream();
    clearTimeout(saveTimer);
    snapRef.current = null;
    clearSnapshot();
    setSnap(null);
    setStale(true);
    setOffline(false);
    go("#pair");
  }, []);

  const apply = useCallback((change) => {
    if (!snapRef.current) return;
    const next = change(snapRef.current);
    snapRef.current = next;
    setSnap(next);
    persist(next);
  }, []);

  const startStream = useCallback(() => {
    stopStream();
    streamRef.current = connect({
      snapshot(data) {
        snapRef.current = data;
        setSnap(data);
        setStale(false);
        setOffline(false);
        persist(data);
        if (data.build && shellBuild && data.build !== shellBuild) upgradeShell(data.build);
        if (!launchDone) {
          launchDone = true;
          launched(data, toast);
        }
        if (routeRef.current.name === "pair") go("#");
      },
      sessions(d) {
        apply((s) => ({ ...s, sessions: { ...s.sessions, [String(d.workspace)]: d.sessions } }));
      },
      workspaces(d) {
        apply((s) => ({ ...s, workspaces: d.workspaces }));
      },
      device(d) {
        apply((s) => ({ ...s, device: d }));
      },
      notice(n) {
        apply((s) => ({ ...s, notices: (s.notices || []).concat([n]).slice(-recentNotices) }));
        toast(n.text, n.error, true);
      },
      error() {
        setStale(true);
        setOffline(true);
      },
      unauthorized() {
        unpair();
      },
    });
  }, []);

  // act calls the API for a screen with path as given: it shows the notices
  // (which stay until tapped) and the errors as toasts, treats a 401 as
  // unpairing, shows nothing for a 202 (the screen says the outcome will
  // follow), and refuses while the stream is down. It resolves
  // {ok, status, data}: ok is true for every 2xx, data is null for a 204.
  const act = useCallback(async (method, path, body) => {
    if (staleRef.current) {
      toast("Not connected. Try again when loom is back.", true);
      return { ok: false, status: 0, data: null };
    }
    const { status, data } = await call(method, path, body);
    if (status === 401) {
      unpair();
      return { ok: false, status, data };
    }
    const ok = status >= 200 && status < 300;
    if (data && Array.isArray(data.notices)) {
      for (const n of data.notices) toast(n.text, n.error, true);
    }
    if (!ok) toast((data && data.error) || "That didn't work.", true);
    if (ok && method === "DELETE" && path.endsWith("/device")) unpair();
    return { ok, status, data };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onHash = () => setRoute(parseRoute(location.hash));
    window.addEventListener("hashchange", onHash);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("hashchange", onHash);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (install) return undefined;
    loadSnapshot().then((cached) => {
      if (cached && !snapRef.current) {
        snapRef.current = cached;
        setSnap(cached);
      }
    });
    startStream();
    return stopStream;
  }, [install]);

  const onPaired = useCallback(() => {
    startStream();
    go("#");
  }, []);

  if (install) {
    return html`<${AddToHome}
      onContinue=${() => {
        pref("browser", true);
        setInstall(false);
      }}
    />`;
  }

  let body;
  if (route.name === "pair") {
    body = html`<${Pair} onPaired=${onPaired} />`;
  } else if (!snap) {
    body = html`<div class="splash">${offline ? "Can't reach loom. Retrying…" : "Connecting…"}</div>`;
  } else {
    const Screen = screens[route.name];
    const props = { snap, stale, route, go, act, toast };
    body = Screen
      ? html`<${Screen} key=${route.name + (route.id === undefined ? "" : route.id)} ...${props} />`
      : html`<div class="splash">Nothing here yet.</div>`;
  }

  return html`
    <div class="shell">
      ${snap && stale && route.name !== "pair"
        ? html`<div class="stale-bar" role="status">Not connected. Showing the last known state.</div>`
        : null}
      <main>${body}</main>
      <div class="toasts" aria-live="polite">
        ${toasts.map(
          (t) => html`<div key=${t.id} class=${"toast" + (t.error ? " error" : "")} role=${t.error ? "alert" : "status"} onClick=${() => dismissToast(t.id)}>${t.text}</div>`,
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

render(html`<${App} />`, document.getElementById("app"));
```

## Story 10: The app's screens

- **Developer:** intermediate-dev
- **Files:** `internal/web/static/js/fleet.mjs`, `internal/web/static/js/session.mjs`, `internal/web/static/js/newsession.mjs`, `internal/web/static/js/sheets.mjs`, `internal/web/static/js/settings.mjs`, `internal/web/static/js/screens.mjs`, `internal/web/static/js/push.mjs`, `internal/web/static/app.css`
- **Interfaces:** exposes `screens` (`fleet`, `session`, `new`, `settings`), `ConfirmSheet`, and the `PushButton` stub in `js/push.mjs` for Story 12; uses the app's module contract (Story 9) and the JSON of Stories 7 and 8
- **Verify:** `CGO_ENABLED=0 go test ./internal/web/... && bash -c 'for f in internal/web/static/js/*.mjs; do node --check "$f" || exit 1; done'`
- **Review:** deep

The screens follow the spec's "The app" section (the mockups were not readable when this was drafted): the fleet grouped by what needs you (Needs you longest wait first, Your turn with a dot when the turn ended after this phone last opened the session, Working, and Paused folded away) with workspace chips whose choice the phone remembers; a session that leads with why it waits, its tool summary and Open in Claude, then its facts, its actions and a reply box; the confirmation sheets for kill, push and merge (pause and resume never ask); the new-session screen with the launch defaults the daemon sends; and settings with the notification toggles, an hour's pause, the recent notices and unpairing. The app has no JavaScript test harness, so each task's logic lives in small pure functions that the task's own check imports under Node (`node --input-type=module -e`, inline, no file in the tree) and its Preact components are covered by `node --check` and the owner's iPhone checklist. Every action button follows the session's `group` and `label` only and is off while `stale`; the daemon's `admit` stays the authority, so a refusal arrives as the 409 text in a toast. Paths passed to `act` are whole (`/api/v1/...`). The story appends to `app.css` and changes nothing above Story 9's rules; every class it adds starts `sc-`, so it cannot collide with the shell's. No landmine marker appears in the story's code.

```js
// Confirmation sheets. ConfirmSheet is the one sheet the screens use: a
// title, a body (text or markup), and a confirm and a cancel button.
// utf8Len is the screens' shared byte count for the 64 KiB limits.
import { html, useState, useEffect } from "../lib/preact-htm.mjs";

export function utf8Len(s) {
  return new TextEncoder().encode(s).length;
}

// onConfirm may return a promise; the confirm button stays off until it
// settles, so a double tap sends one request. The caller closes the sheet.
export function ConfirmSheet({ title, body, confirm, danger, onConfirm, onCancel }) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onCancel();
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [onCancel]);

  async function run() {
    if (busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await onConfirm();
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return html`
    <div class="sc-backdrop" onClick=${() => { if (!busy) onCancel(); }}>
      <div
        class="sc-sheet"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label=${title}
        onClick=${(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <h2 class="sc-sheet-title">${title}</h2>
        <div class="sc-sheet-body">${body}</div>
        <div class="sc-sheet-actions">
          <button class="sc-btn" type="button" disabled=${busy} onClick=${onCancel}>Cancel</button>
          <button
            class=${"sc-btn " + (danger ? "sc-danger" : "sc-primary")}
            type="button"
            disabled=${busy}
            onClick=${run}
          >${confirm}</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}
```

```css
.sc-btn {
```

Create `internal/web/static/js/fleet.mjs`:

```js
// The fleet screen, and the helpers the session screen shares with it.
import { html, useState, useEffect } from "../lib/preact-htm.mjs";
import { pref } from "./store.mjs";

export const GROUPS = [
  { key: "needs_you", title: "Needs you" },
  { key: "your_turn", title: "Your turn" },
  { key: "working", title: "Working" },
  { key: "paused", title: "Paused" },
];

export function allSessions(snap) {
  return Object.values((snap && snap.sessions) || {}).flat();
}

// groupSessions returns {needs_you, your_turn, working, paused}, each an
// array: Needs you with the longest wait first, the others newest first.
// A filter of "" keeps every workspace.
export function groupSessions(sessions, filter) {
  const out = { needs_you: [], your_turn: [], working: [], paused: [] };
  for (const s of sessions) {
    if (filter !== "" && String(s.workspace) !== filter) continue;
    if (out[s.group]) out[s.group].push(s);
  }
  const t = (s) => Date.parse(s.since) || 0;
  out.needs_you.sort((a, b) => t(a) - t(b));
  for (const k of ["your_turn", "working", "paused"]) out[k].sort((a, b) => t(b) - t(a));
  return out;
}

// readSeen is {"<session id>": ms}: when this phone last opened the session.
export function readSeen() {
  try {
    const v = JSON.parse(pref("seen") || "{}");
    return v && typeof v === "object" ? v : {};
  } catch (e) {
    return {};
  }
}

export function markSeen(id) {
  const seen = readSeen();
  seen[id] = Date.now();
  pref("seen", JSON.stringify(seen));
}

// pruneSeen drops the times of sessions the daemon no longer lists. An
// empty list prunes nothing: it is more likely not loaded than a fleet of none.
export function pruneSeen(ids) {
  if (ids.length === 0) return;
  const keep = new Set(ids.map(String));
  const seen = readSeen();
  let changed = false;
  for (const k of Object.keys(seen)) {
    if (!keep.has(k)) {
      delete seen[k];
      changed = true;
    }
  }
  if (changed) pref("seen", JSON.stringify(seen));
}

// hasNewTurn: a Your turn session whose turn ended after this phone last
// opened it, or that it never opened.
export function hasNewTurn(s, seen) {
  if (s.group !== "your_turn") return false;
  const opened = seen[s.id];
  return opened === undefined || (Date.parse(s.since) || 0) > opened;
}

export function ago(iso, now = Date.now()) {
  const t = Date.parse(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(t)) return "";
  const secs = Math.max(0, Math.round((now - t) / 1000));
  if (secs < 60) return "now";
  const mins = Math.floor(secs / 60);
  if (mins < 60) return mins + "m";
  const hours = Math.floor(mins / 60);
  if (hours < 48) return hours + "h";
  return Math.floor(hours / 24) + "d";
}

export function diffText(s) {
  const d = s.diff;
  if (!d || (!d.added && !d.removed)) return "";
  return "+" + d.added + " −" + d.removed;
}

export function prText(s) {
  const pr = s.pr;
  if (!pr) return "";
  const checks = pr.checks && pr.checks !== "none" ? ", checks " + pr.checks : "";
  return "PR #" + pr.number + " " + pr.state + checks;
}

function Card({ s, chip, dot }) {
  const reason = s.group === "needs_you" && s.wait ? s.wait.reason : "";
  const detail = s.group === "needs_you" && s.wait ? s.wait.detail : "";
  const meta = [s.label, diffText(s), prText(s)].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
  return html`
    <a class=${"sc-card sc-g-" + s.group} href=${"#s/" + s.id}>
      <div class="sc-card-top">
        <span class="sc-card-title">${s.title}</span>
        ${dot ? html`<span class="sc-dot" role="img" aria-label="New turn"></span>` : null}
        <span class="sc-age">${ago(s.since)}</span>
      </div>
      ${chip ? html`<span class="sc-chip">${chip}</span>` : null}
      ${reason ? html`<div class="sc-wait">${reason}</div>` : null}
      ${detail ? html`<div class="sc-detail">${detail}</div>` : null}
      ${!reason && s.last_message ? html`<div class="sc-last">${s.last_message}</div>` : null}
      <div class="sc-meta">${meta}</div>
    </a>
  `;
}

export function Fleet({ snap, stale, go }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState(() => pref("filter") || "");
  const [, setTick] = useState(0);

  // Ages and the dot move with the clock, not only with the stream.
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setInterval(() => setTick((n) => n + 1), 30000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, []);

  const everything = allSessions(snap);
  useEffect(() => {
    pruneSeen(everything.map((s) => s.id));
  }, [snap]);

  if (!snap) return html`<main class="sc-screen"><p class="sc-dim">Loading…</p></main>`;

  const workspaces = snap.workspaces || [];
  const known = new Set(workspaces.map((w) => String(w.id)));
  const active = known.has(filter) ? filter : "";
  const names = new Map(workspaces.map((w) => [String(w.id), w.label || w.name]));
  const groups = groupSessions(everything, active);
  const seen = readSeen();
  const shown = GROUPS.reduce((n, g) => n + groups[g.key].length, 0);

  function pick(id) {
    setFilter(id);
    pref("filter", id);
  }

  const renderCards = (list) =>
    list.map(
      (s) => html`<${Card}
        key=${s.id}
        s=${s}
        chip=${active === "" && workspaces.length > 1 ? names.get(String(s.workspace)) : ""}
        dot=${hasNewTurn(s, seen)}
      />`,
    );

  return html`
    <main class="sc-screen">
      <header class="sc-header">
        <h1 class="sc-h1">${snap.host || "loom"}</h1>
        <a class="sc-icon" href="#settings" aria-label="Settings">⚙</a>
        <button class="sc-btn sc-primary" type="button" disabled=${stale} onClick=${() => go("#new")}>New</button>
      </header>
      ${workspaces.length > 1
        ? html`<nav class="sc-chips" aria-label="Workspaces">
            <button class=${"sc-chip-btn" + (active === "" ? " sc-on" : "")} type="button" onClick=${() => pick("")}>All</button>
            ${workspaces.map(
              (w) => html`<button
                key=${w.id}
                class=${"sc-chip-btn" + (active === String(w.id) ? " sc-on" : "")}
                type="button"
                onClick=${() => pick(String(w.id))}
              >${w.label || w.name}</button>`,
            )}
          </nav>`
        : null}
      ${shown === 0 ? html`<p class="sc-dim sc-empty">No sessions${active ? " in this workspace" : ""}.</p>` : null}
      ${GROUPS.filter((g) => g.key !== "paused" && groups[g.key].length > 0).map(
        (g) => html`<section key=${g.key} class="sc-group">
          <h2 class=${"sc-group-title sc-t-" + g.key}>${g.title} <span class="sc-count">${groups[g.key].length}</span></h2>
          ${renderCards(groups[g.key])}
        </section>`,
      )}
      ${groups.paused.length > 0
        ? html`<details class="sc-group">
            <summary class="sc-group-title sc-t-paused">Paused <span class="sc-count">${groups.paused.length}</span></summary>
            ${renderCards(groups.paused)}
          </details>`
        : null}
    </main>
  `;
}
```

```js
// The session screen: why it waits, Open in Claude, its facts, the
// actions and the reply box.
import { html, useState, useEffect } from "../lib/preact-htm.mjs";
import { ConfirmSheet, utf8Len } from "./sheets.mjs";
import { allSessions, ago, diffText, prText, markSeen } from "./fleet.mjs";

export const MAX_REPLY = 64 * 1024;

// actionsFor says which buttons to show. It follows status only; the
// daemon's admit stays the authority and a refusal comes back as a toast.
export function actionsFor(s) {
  const busy = s.label === "Loading" || s.label === "Deleting";
  const paused = s.group === "paused";
  return {
    pause: !busy && !paused,
    resume: !busy && paused,
    push: !busy && !paused,
    merge: !busy && !paused,
    send: !busy && !paused,
    kill: s.label !== "Deleting",
  };
}

// mergeCandidates: the other sessions of the same workspace, whose branch
// can be merged into this one.
export function mergeCandidates(snap, s) {
  return allSessions(snap).filter((o) => o.workspace === s.workspace && o.id !== s.id);
}

export function Session({ snap, stale, route, go, act, toast }) {
  const id = route.id;
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState("");
  const [source, setSource] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  // The time this phone last opened the session feeds the fleet's dot; it
  // is written on arrival and again on leaving, so a turn that ended while
  // the session was open does not light the dot.
  useEffect(() => {
    setText("");
    setSheet("");
    if (id === undefined) return undefined;
    markSeen(id);
    return () => markSeen(id);
  }, [id]);

  const s = snap && id !== undefined ? allSessions(snap).find((x) => x.id === id) : undefined;
  if (!s) {
    return html`
      <main class="sc-screen">
        <a class="sc-back" href="#">‹ Fleet</a>
        <p class="sc-dim sc-empty">${snap ? "This session is gone." : "Loading…"}</p>
      </main>
    `;
  }

  const can = actionsFor(s);
  const off = stale || busy;
  const base = "/api/v1/sessions/" + s.id;
  const candidates = mergeCandidates(snap, s);
  const reply = text.trim();
  const tooLong = utf8Len(reply) > MAX_REPLY;

  async function run(action, body) {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const r = await act("POST", base + "/" + action, body);
      if (r.status === 202) toast("Still running; the result will show here.", false);
      return r;
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function send() {
    const r = await run("send", { text: reply });
    if (r.ok && r.status !== 202) setText("");
  }

  async function kill() {
    const r = await run("kill");
    setSheet("");
    if (r.ok && r.status !== 202) go("#");
  }

  async function pushBranch() {
    await run("push");
    setSheet("");
  }

  async function merge() {
    await run("merge", { source: Number(source) });
    setSheet("");
  }

  function openMerge() {
    if (candidates.length === 0) {
      toast("No other session in this workspace to merge in.", true);
      return;
    }
    setSource(String(candidates[0].id));
    setSheet("merge");
  }

  const waiting = s.group === "needs_you";
  const facts = [
    ["Status", s.status],
    ["Since", ago(s.since)],
    ["Changes", diffText(s)],
    ["Branch", s.branch],
    ["Ahead / behind", s.parity_known ? s.ahead + " ahead, " + s.behind + " behind" : ""],
    ["Pull request", prText(s)],
    ["Account", s.account],
    ["Agents", s.agents.length > 0 ? s.agents.length + " running" : ""],
  ].filter(([, v]) => v);

  return html`
    <main class="sc-screen sc-session">
      <a class="sc-back" href="#">‹ Fleet</a>
      <h1 class="sc-h1 sc-wrap">${s.title}</h1>
      <div class=${"sc-lead sc-g-" + s.group}>
        <div class=${"sc-lead-label" + (waiting ? " sc-attn" : "")}>
          ${waiting ? (s.wait && s.wait.reason) || "Needs you" : s.label}
        </div>
        ${waiting && s.wait && s.wait.detail ? html`<pre class="sc-detail-box">${s.wait.detail}</pre>` : null}
        ${s.remote_url
          ? html`<a class="sc-btn sc-primary sc-block" href=${s.remote_url} target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open in Claude</a>`
          : html`<p class="sc-dim sc-small">Remote Control is off for this session.</p>`}
      </div>

      ${s.last_message
        ? html`<section class="sc-section">
            <h2 class="sc-group-title">Last message</h2>
            <p class="sc-pre">${s.last_message}</p>
          </section>`
        : null}

      <section class="sc-section">
        <h2 class="sc-group-title">Details</h2>
        <dl class="sc-facts">
          ${facts.map(([k, v]) => html`<div key=${k} class="sc-fact"><dt>${k}</dt><dd>${v}</dd></div>`)}
        </dl>
      </section>

      <section class="sc-actions">
        ${can.pause ? html`<button class="sc-btn" type="button" disabled=${off} onClick=${() => run("pause")}>Pause</button>` : null}
        ${can.resume ? html`<button class="sc-btn sc-primary" type="button" disabled=${off} onClick=${() => run("resume")}>Resume</button>` : null}
        ${can.push ? html`<button class="sc-btn" type="button" disabled=${off} onClick=${() => setSheet("push")}>Push</button>` : null}
        ${can.merge ? html`<button class="sc-btn" type="button" disabled=${off} onClick=${openMerge}>Merge</button>` : null}
        ${can.kill ? html`<button class="sc-btn sc-danger-text" type="button" disabled=${off} onClick=${() => setSheet("kill")}>Kill</button>` : null}
      </section>

      ${can.send
        ? html`<form class="sc-reply" onSubmit=${(e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (!off && reply && !tooLong) send(); }}>
            <textarea
              rows="3"
              placeholder="Reply to Claude…"
              value=${text}
              onInput=${(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
            ></textarea>
            ${tooLong ? html`<div class="sc-small sc-err">Too long: a reply is at most 64 KiB.</div>` : null}
            <button class="sc-btn sc-primary" type="submit" disabled=${off || !reply || tooLong}>Send</button>
          </form>`
        : s.group === "paused"
          ? html`<p class="sc-dim sc-small">Resume the session to reply.</p>`
          : null}

      ${sheet === "kill"
        ? html`<${ConfirmSheet}
            title="Kill this session?"
            confirm="Kill"
            danger=${true}
            body=${html`<p>This removes the session's worktree and its tmux session, and deletes its branch <code>${s.branch}</code>. Uncommitted changes are stashed first, and the notice names the stash (Settings, Recent notices).</p>`}
            onConfirm=${kill}
            onCancel=${() => setSheet("")}
          />`
        : null}
      ${sheet === "push"
        ? html`<${ConfirmSheet}
            title="Push this branch?"
            confirm="Commit and push"
            body=${html`<p>This commits the session's uncommitted changes first, then pushes <code>${s.branch}</code>.</p>`}
            onConfirm=${pushBranch}
            onCancel=${() => setSheet("")}
          />`
        : null}
      ${sheet === "merge"
        ? html`<${ConfirmSheet}
            title="Merge a branch in"
            confirm="Merge"
            body=${html`<p>Merge which session's branch into <code>${s.branch}</code>?</p>
              <select value=${source} onChange=${(e) => setSource(e.target.value)} aria-label="Session to merge in">
                ${candidates.map((o) => html`<option key=${o.id} value=${String(o.id)}>${o.title} (${o.branch})</option>`)}
              </select>`}
            onConfirm=${merge}
            onCancel=${() => setSheet("")}
          />`
        : null}
    </main>
  `;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/web/static/js/newsession.mjs`:

```js
// The new-session screen. The daemon sends the launch defaults and the
// lists to choose from; the app hard-codes none of them and sends a field
// only when it differs from its default. The daemon's checks stay the authority.
import { html, useState, useEffect } from "../lib/preact-htm.mjs";
import { pref } from "./store.mjs";
import { utf8Len } from "./sheets.mjs";

const MAX_PROMPT = 64 * 1024;

// choices is the list with the default among it, first when the list lacks it.
export function choices(list, def) {
  const out = (list || []).slice();
  if (!out.includes(def)) out.unshift(def);
  return out;
}

export function choiceLabel(v) {
  return v === "" ? "(default)" : v;
}

// launchBody builds the POST /api/v1/sessions body: the workspace, the
// trimmed title and prompt, and each launch override that differs from its default.
export function launchBody({ workspace, title, prompt, defaults, picks }) {
  const body = { workspace, title: title.trim(), prompt: prompt.trim() };
  for (const k of ["account", "model", "permission_mode", "effort"]) {
    if (picks[k] !== defaults[k]) body[k] = picks[k];
  }
  return body;
}

function Pick({ label, list, def, value, onChange }) {
  return html`
    <label class="sc-field">
      <span>${label}</span>
      <select value=${value} onChange=${(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}>
        ${choices(list, def).map((v) => html`<option key=${v} value=${v}>${choiceLabel(v)}</option>`)}
      </select>
    </label>
  `;
}

export function NewSession({ snap, stale, go, act }) {
  const workspaces = (snap && snap.workspaces) || [];
  const [ws, setWs] = useState(() => {
    const want = pref("filter") || "";
    const hit = workspaces.find((w) => String(w.id) === want);
    return String(hit ? hit.id : workspaces.length > 0 ? workspaces[0].id : "");
  });
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState("");
  const [defs, setDefs] = useState(null);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);
  const [picks, setPicks] = useState({ account: "", model: "", permission_mode: "", effort: "" });
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  // The defaults are per workspace (its settings and accounts), so they are
  // fetched again when the workspace changes.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ws === "") return undefined;
    let live = true;
    setDefs(null);
    setFailed(false);
    act("GET", "/api/v1/launch-defaults?workspace=" + encodeURIComponent(ws)).then((r) => {
      if (!live) return;
      if (!r.ok || !r.data) {
        setFailed(true);
        return;
      }
      setDefs(r.data);
      setPicks({
        account: r.data.account,
        model: r.data.model,
        permission_mode: r.data.permission_mode,
        effort: r.data.effort,
      });
    });
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [ws, attempt]);

  if (!snap) return html`<main class="sc-screen"><p class="sc-dim">Loading…</p></main>`;

  const chosen = workspaces.find((w) => String(w.id) === ws);
  const promptTooLong = utf8Len(prompt.trim()) > MAX_PROMPT;
  const ready = !stale && !busy && defs && chosen && title.trim() !== "" && !promptTooLong;
  const set = (k) => (v) => setPicks((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: v }));

  async function create() {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const r = await act(
        "POST",
        "/api/v1/sessions",
        launchBody({ workspace: chosen.id, title, prompt, defaults: defs, picks }),
      );
      if (!r.ok) return;
      if (r.status === 201 && r.data && r.data.id !== undefined) go("#s/" + r.data.id);
      else go("#");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return html`
    <main class="sc-screen">
      <a class="sc-back" href="#">‹ Fleet</a>
      <h1 class="sc-h1 sc-wrap">New session</h1>
      <form class="sc-form" onSubmit=${(e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (ready) create(); }}>
        <label class="sc-field">
          <span>Workspace</span>
          <select value=${ws} onChange=${(e) => setWs(e.target.value)}>
            ${workspaces.map((w) => html`<option key=${w.id} value=${String(w.id)}>${w.label || w.name}</option>`)}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label class="sc-field">
          <span>Title</span>
          <input type="text" value=${title} autocapitalize="none" autocomplete="off" onInput=${(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} />
        </label>
        <label class="sc-field">
          <span>Prompt</span>
          <textarea rows="6" value=${prompt} onInput=${(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)}></textarea>
          ${promptTooLong ? html`<span class="sc-small sc-err">Too long: a prompt is at most 64 KiB.</span>` : null}
        </label>
        ${failed
          ? html`<p class="sc-small sc-err">
              Could not load the launch defaults.
              <button class="sc-btn" type="button" onClick=${() => setAttempt((n) => n + 1)}>Retry</button>
            </p>`
          : null}
        ${defs
          ? html`<details class="sc-launch">
              <summary>Launch options</summary>
              ${defs.accounts && defs.accounts.length > 0
                ? html`<${Pick} label="Account" list=${defs.accounts} def=${defs.account} value=${picks.account} onChange=${set("account")} />`
                : null}
              <${Pick} label="Model" list=${defs.models} def=${defs.model} value=${picks.model} onChange=${set("model")} />
              <${Pick} label="Permission mode" list=${defs.permission_modes} def=${defs.permission_mode} value=${picks.permission_mode} onChange=${set("permission_mode")} />
              <${Pick} label="Effort" list=${defs.efforts} def=${defs.effort} value=${picks.effort} onChange=${set("effort")} />
            </details>`
          : null}
        <button class="sc-btn sc-primary sc-block" type="submit" disabled=${!ready}>Start session</button>
      </form>
    </main>
  `;
}
```

Create `internal/web/static/js/settings.mjs`:

```js
// The settings screen: notifications, an hour's pause, the recent notices
// and unpairing. The device state comes from the stream's snapshot and from
// each PUT's answer, which is the device as stored.
import { html, useState, useEffect } from "../lib/preact-htm.mjs";
import { ConfirmSheet } from "./sheets.mjs";
import { PushButton } from "./push.mjs";
import { clearSnapshot } from "./store.mjs";
import { ago } from "./fleet.mjs";

export function isPaused(device, now = Date.now()) {
  const until = Date.parse(device && device.notify ? device.notify.paused_until : "");
  return !Number.isNaN(until) && until > now;
}

// pushMessage is what the card says when the push subscription stopped
// working, and "" while it works or was never made.
export function pushMessage(device) {
  const state = (device && device.push) || "";
  if (state === "expired") return "Notifications stopped; turn them on again.";
  if (state.startsWith("failing")) return "Notifications are failing (" + state + "); turn them on again.";
  return "";
}

function clock(iso) {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit" });
}

export function Settings({ snap, stale, go, act, toast }) {
  const [stored, setStored] = useState(null);
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState(false);

  // A fresh device from the stream replaces the last PUT's answer.
  useEffect(() => {
    setStored(null);
  }, [snap && snap.device]);

  if (!snap || !snap.device) return html`<main class="sc-screen"><p class="sc-dim">Loading…</p></main>`;

  const device = stored || snap.device;
  const notify = device.notify || {};
  const paused = isPaused(device);
  const message = pushMessage(device);
  const notices = (snap.notices || []).slice().reverse();

  async function put(body) {
    const r = await act("PUT", "/api/v1/device", body);
    if (r.ok && r.data) setStored(r.data);
    return r;
  }

  // The checkbox is uncontrolled between renders, so a refused change puts
  // it back by hand.
  function toggle(key) {
    return async (e) => {
      const el = e.target;
      const r = await put({ [key]: el.checked });
      el.checked = !!((r.ok && r.data ? r.data.notify : notify)[key]);
    };
  }

  async function unpair() {
    const r = await act("DELETE", "/api/v1/device");
    if (!r.ok) return;
    await clearSnapshot();
    go("#pair");
  }

  return html`
    <main class="sc-screen">
      <a class="sc-back" href="#">‹ Fleet</a>
      <h1 class="sc-h1 sc-wrap">Settings</h1>

      <section class="sc-section">
        <h2 class="sc-group-title">Notifications</h2>
        <${PushButton} device=${device} act=${act} toast=${toast} />
        ${message ? html`<p class="sc-small sc-err">${message}</p>` : null}
        <label class="sc-row">
          <span>When a session needs you</span>
          <input type="checkbox" checked=${!!notify.waiting} disabled=${stale} onChange=${toggle("waiting")} />
        </label>
        <label class="sc-row">
          <span>When a session finishes</span>
          <input type="checkbox" checked=${!!notify.finished} disabled=${stale} onChange=${toggle("finished")} />
        </label>
        <div class="sc-row">
          <span>${paused ? "Paused until " + clock(notify.paused_until) : "Not paused"}</span>
          ${paused
            ? html`<button class="sc-btn" type="button" disabled=${stale} onClick=${() => put({ pause: "none" })}>Resume now</button>`
            : html`<button class="sc-btn" type="button" disabled=${stale} onClick=${() => put({ pause: "1h" })}>Pause for an hour</button>`}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="sc-section">
        <h2 class="sc-group-title">This phone</h2>
        <div class="sc-row"><span>Paired as</span><span>${device.name}</span></div>
        <div class="sc-row"><span>Host</span><span>${snap.host}</span></div>
        <div class="sc-row"><span>Build</span><span class="sc-mono">${snap.build}</span></div>
        <button class="sc-btn sc-danger-text sc-block" type="button" disabled=${stale} onClick=${() => setSheet(true)}>Unpair this phone</button>
      </section>

      <section class="sc-section">
        <h2 class="sc-group-title">Recent notices</h2>
        ${notices.length === 0
          ? html`<p class="sc-dim sc-small">None.</p>`
          : notices.map(
              (n, i) => html`<div key=${i} class=${"sc-notice" + (n.error ? " sc-err" : "")}>
                <span class="sc-age">${ago(n.at)}</span> ${n.text}
              </div>`,
            )}
      </section>

      ${sheet
        ? html`<${ConfirmSheet}
            title="Unpair this phone?"
            confirm="Unpair"
            danger=${true}
            body=${html`<p>This phone stops getting notifications and goes back to pairing. Pair it again with <code>loom web pair</code> on the host.</p>`}
            onConfirm=${unpair}
            onCancel=${() => setSheet(false)}
          />`
        : null}
    </main>
  `;
}
```

## Story 11: Web Push's crypto: VAPID and RFC 8291 encryption

- **Developer:** intermediate-dev
- **Files:** `internal/web/webpush/vapid.go`, `internal/web/webpush/encrypt.go`, `internal/web/webpush/request.go`, `internal/web/webpush/webpush_test.go`
- **Interfaces:** exposes the webpush names of the contract; uses `core.VAPIDKey` and `core.PushSubscription` (Story 3)
- **Verify:** `CGO_ENABLED=0 go test ./internal/web/webpush/`
- **Review:** deep

A pure package, `internal/web/webpush`, that Story 12's sender builds on: it parses a stored subscription, encrypts a payload as one RFC 8291 `aes128gcm` record (checked against the RFC's own example, Appendix A), signs the RFC 8292 VAPID header (checked by verifying the JWT with its public key, and its signing input against RFC 8292's example), and assembles the RFC 8030 push request with its `TTL`, `Urgency` and `Topic` headers, refusing a `Topic` whose length is 1 mod 4, which Apple answers with 400 `BadWebPushTopic` (the spike). It holds no state and does no network I/O, and it uses only the standard library (`crypto/ecdh`, `crypto/ecdsa`, `crypto/hkdf`, `crypto/aes`, `crypto/cipher`), so `go.mod` and `vendor/` do not change. Its tests reach `core` (for `core.VAPIDKey` and `core.PushSubscription`), so `webpush_test.go` carries the `TestMain` that calls `testenv.MustIsolateLoomDirs`, which `TestEveryConfigReachingPackageIsolatesLoomDirs` requires. The package is covered by `internal/web/CLAUDE.md` (Story 6), so it needs no `CLAUDE.md` of its own. No landmine marker appears in its code.

Two unexported seams exist for the tests and nothing else: `encryptWith(sub, payload, sender, salt)`, which `Encrypt` calls with a random sender key and salt so the RFC's fixed values can be fed in, and `contentKeys(...)`, the RFC 8291 section 3.3 and RFC 8188 derivation, whose intermediate values the RFC prints. The RFC 8291 values below are copied from its Appendix A and section 5 (`curl -s https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8291.txt`); their line breaks and spaces are dropped, and the test's `b64` helper drops any that remain.

### Task 11.1: Parse a subscription and encrypt a payload (RFC 8291)

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/web/webpush/encrypt.go`
- Test: `internal/web/webpush/webpush_test.go`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `internal/web/webpush/webpush_test.go`:

```go
package webpush

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/aes"
	"crypto/cipher"
	"crypto/ecdh"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/binary"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/aidan-bailey/loom/core"
	"github.com/aidan-bailey/loom/internal/testenv"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

// TestMain points LOOM_HOME and LOOM_GLOBAL_DIR at throwaway directories:
// this package imports core, so its tests can reach config.
func TestMain(m *testing.M) {
	cleanup := testenv.MustIsolateLoomDirs()
	code := m.Run()
	cleanup()
	os.Exit(code)
}

// RFC 8291, Appendix A ("Intermediate Values for Encryption") and section 5
// ("Push Message Encryption Example"), base64url without padding.
const (
	rfcPlaintext  = "V2hlbiBJIGdyb3cgdXAsIEkgd2FudCB0byBiZSBhIHdhdGVybWVsb24"
	rfcAuthSecret = "BTBZMqHH6r4Tts7J_aSIgg"
	rfcUAPrivate  = "q1dXpw3UpT5VOmu_cf_v6ih07Aems3njxI-JWgLcM94"
	rfcUAPublic   = "BCVxsr7N_eNgVRqvHtD0zTZsEc6-VV-JvLexhqUzORcxaOzi6-AYWXvTBHm4bjyPjs7Vd8pZGH6SRpkNtoIAiw4"
	rfcASPrivate  = "yfWPiYE-n46HLnH0KqZOF1fJJU3MYrct3AELtAQ-oRw"
	rfcASPublic   = "BP4z9KsN6nGRTbVYI_c7VJSPQTBtkgcy27mlmlMoZIIgDll6e3vCYLocInmYWAmS6TlzAC8wEqKK6PBru3jl7A8"
	rfcSalt       = "DGv6ra1nlYgDCS1FRnbzlw"
	rfcECDHSecret = "kyrL1jIIOHEzg3sM2ZWRHDRB62YACZhhSlknJ672kSs"
	rfcCEK        = "oIhVW04MRdy2XN9CiKLxTg"
	rfcNonce      = "4h_95klXJ5E_qnoN"
	rfcBody       = "DGv6ra1nlYgDCS1FRnbzlwAAEABBBP4z9KsN6nGRTbVYI_c7VJSPQTBtkgcy27mlmlMoZIIgDll6e3vCYLocInmYWAmS6TlzAC8wEqKK6PBru3jl7A_yl95bQpu6cVPTpK4Mqgkf1CXztLVBSt2Ks3oZwbuwXPXLWyouBWLVWGNWQexSgSxsj_Qulcy4a-fN"
)

// b64 decodes unpadded base64url, dropping the whitespace the RFCs wrap it with.
func b64(t *testing.T, s string) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	b, err := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(strings.Join(strings.Fields(s), ""))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return b
}

func b64s(b []byte) string { return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(b) }

// newSub makes a subscription the way a browser does, and returns the
// receiver's private key so a test can open what Encrypt made.
func newSub(t *testing.T) (Subscription, *ecdh.PrivateKey) {
	t.Helper()
	key, err := ecdh.P256().GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	auth := make([]byte, authLen)
	_, err = io.ReadFull(rand.Reader, auth)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return Subscription{
		Endpoint: "https://web.push.apple.com/wpush/v2/abc",
		P256DH:   key.PublicKey().Bytes(),
		Auth:     auth,
	}, key
}

// open is the user agent's side of RFC 8291: it parses the header, derives
// the keys from the receiver's private key and decrypts the one record.
func open(receiver *ecdh.PrivateKey, auth, body []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	if len(body) < headerLen+1+tagLen {
		return nil, errors.New("body shorter than a header and an empty record")
	}
	salt := body[:saltLen]
	if rs := binary.BigEndian.Uint32(body[saltLen : saltLen+4]); rs != recordSize {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("record size %d", rs)
	}
	if body[saltLen+4] != pointLen {
		return nil, errors.New("key ID is not a P-256 point")
	}
	keyID := body[saltLen+5 : headerLen]
	sender, err := ecdh.P256().NewPublicKey(keyID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	secret, err := receiver.ECDH(sender)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	cek, nonce, err := contentKeys(secret, auth, receiver.PublicKey().Bytes(), keyID, salt)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	block, err := aes.NewCipher(cek)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	gcm, err := cipher.NewGCM(block)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	plain, err := gcm.Open(nil, nonce, body[headerLen:], nil)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if len(plain) == 0 || plain[len(plain)-1] != paddingDelim {
		return nil, errors.New("padding delimiter missing")
	}
	return plain[:len(plain)-1], nil
}

func TestEncryptWith_RFC8291Example(t *testing.T) {
	receiver, err := ecdh.P256().NewPrivateKey(b64(t, rfcUAPrivate))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Equal(t, b64(t, rfcUAPublic), receiver.PublicKey().Bytes(), "the RFC's receiver key pair is consistent")
	sender, err := ecdh.P256().NewPrivateKey(b64(t, rfcASPrivate))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Equal(t, b64(t, rfcASPublic), sender.PublicKey().Bytes(), "the RFC's sender key pair is consistent")
	plaintext := b64(t, rfcPlaintext)
	require.Equal(t, "When I grow up, I want to be a watermelon", string(plaintext))

	secret, err := sender.ECDH(receiver.PublicKey())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, b64(t, rfcECDHSecret), secret, "the ECDH secret")

	cek, nonce, err := contentKeys(secret, b64(t, rfcAuthSecret), receiver.PublicKey().Bytes(), sender.PublicKey().Bytes(), b64(t, rfcSalt))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, b64(t, rfcCEK), cek, "the content encryption key")
	assert.Equal(t, b64(t, rfcNonce), nonce, "the nonce")

	sub := Subscription{
		Endpoint: "https://push.example.net/push/JzLQ3raZJfFBR0aqvOMsLrt54w4rJUsV",
		P256DH:   b64(t, rfcUAPublic),
		Auth:     b64(t, rfcAuthSecret),
	}
	body, err := encryptWith(sub, plaintext, sender, b64(t, rfcSalt))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, b64(t, rfcBody), body, "the whole push message body")

	got, err := open(receiver, sub.Auth, b64(t, rfcBody))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, plaintext, got, "the RFC's own body opens with the receiver's key")
}

func TestEncryptWith_DependsOnEveryInput(t *testing.T) {
	receiver, err := ecdh.P256().NewPrivateKey(b64(t, rfcUAPrivate))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	sender, err := ecdh.P256().NewPrivateKey(b64(t, rfcASPrivate))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	other, err := ecdh.P256().GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	plaintext := b64(t, rfcPlaintext)
	sub := Subscription{Endpoint: "https://push.example.net/p", P256DH: receiver.PublicKey().Bytes(), Auth: b64(t, rfcAuthSecret)}
	want := b64(t, rfcBody)

	t.Run("auth secret", func(t *testing.T) {
		s := sub
		s.Auth = bytes.Clone(sub.Auth)
		s.Auth[0] ^= 1
		got, err := encryptWith(s, plaintext, sender, b64(t, rfcSalt))
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.NotEqual(t, want, got)
	})
	t.Run("salt", func(t *testing.T) {
		salt := bytes.Clone(b64(t, rfcSalt))
		salt[0] ^= 1
		got, err := encryptWith(sub, plaintext, sender, salt)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.NotEqual(t, want, got)
	})
	t.Run("sender key", func(t *testing.T) {
		got, err := encryptWith(sub, plaintext, other, b64(t, rfcSalt))
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.NotEqual(t, want, got)
	})
	t.Run("payload", func(t *testing.T) {
		got, err := encryptWith(sub, append(bytes.Clone(plaintext), '!'), sender, b64(t, rfcSalt))
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.NotEqual(t, want, got)
	})
	t.Run("salt of the wrong length", func(t *testing.T) {
		_, err := encryptWith(sub, plaintext, sender, b64(t, rfcSalt)[:15])
		assert.Error(t, err)
	})
}

func TestEncrypt_RoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	sub, receiver := newSub(t)
	payload := []byte(`{"kind":"waiting","session":7}`)

	body, err := Encrypt(sub, payload)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, headerLen+len(payload)+1+tagLen, len(body), "one record: header, payload, delimiter, tag")
	assert.Equal(t, []byte{0x00, 0x00, 0x10, 0x00}, body[saltLen:saltLen+4], "record size 4096")
	assert.Equal(t, byte(65), body[saltLen+4], "key ID length")
	assert.Equal(t, byte(4), body[saltLen+5], "the key ID is an uncompressed point")
	got, err := open(receiver, sub.Auth, body)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, payload, got)

	again, err := Encrypt(sub, payload)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.NotEqual(t, body[:saltLen], again[:saltLen], "a fresh salt each time")
	assert.NotEqual(t, body[saltLen+5:headerLen], again[saltLen+5:headerLen], "a fresh sender key each time")
	got, err = open(receiver, sub.Auth, again)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, payload, got)
}

func TestEncrypt_OnlyTheReceiverCanOpenIt(t *testing.T) {
	sub, receiver := newSub(t)
	_, stranger := newSub(t)
	body, err := Encrypt(sub, []byte("secret"))
	require.NoError(t, err)

	_, err = open(receiver, sub.Auth, body)
	require.NoError(t, err, "control: the receiver opens it")

	_, err = open(stranger, sub.Auth, body)
	assert.Error(t, err, "another key pair")
	wrongAuth := bytes.Clone(sub.Auth)
	wrongAuth[0] ^= 1
	_, err = open(receiver, wrongAuth, body)
	assert.Error(t, err, "another authentication secret")
	for _, i := range []int{0, saltLen + 4, saltLen + 20, headerLen + 2, len(body) - 1} {
		bad := bytes.Clone(body)
		bad[i] ^= 1
		_, err = open(receiver, sub.Auth, bad)
		assert.Error(t, err, "byte %d flipped", i)
	}
}

func TestEncrypt_PayloadLimit(t *testing.T) {
	sub, receiver := newSub(t)

	full := bytes.Repeat([]byte("x"), maxPayload)
	body, err := Encrypt(sub, full)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, recordSize, len(body), "3993 bytes of payload fill the 4096-byte record exactly")
	got, err := open(receiver, sub.Auth, body)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, full, got)

	_, err = Encrypt(sub, bytes.Repeat([]byte("x"), maxPayload+1))
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, errPayloadTooLarge)

	empty, err := Encrypt(sub, nil)
	require.NoError(t, err, "an empty payload is one delimiter and a tag")
	got, err = open(receiver, sub.Auth, empty)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Empty(t, got)
}

func TestEncrypt_RefusesABadSubscription(t *testing.T) {
	good, _ := newSub(t)
	offCurve := bytes.Clone(good.P256DH)
	offCurve[len(offCurve)-1] ^= 0xff
	compressed := bytes.Clone(good.P256DH)
	compressed[0] = 0x02

	for name, sub := range map[string]Subscription{
		"no key":           {Endpoint: good.Endpoint, Auth: good.Auth},
		"short key":        {Endpoint: good.Endpoint, P256DH: good.P256DH[:64], Auth: good.Auth},
		"long key":         {Endpoint: good.Endpoint, P256DH: append(bytes.Clone(good.P256DH), 0), Auth: good.Auth},
		"point off curve":  {Endpoint: good.Endpoint, P256DH: offCurve, Auth: good.Auth},
		"not uncompressed": {Endpoint: good.Endpoint, P256DH: compressed, Auth: good.Auth},
		"no auth":          {Endpoint: good.Endpoint, P256DH: good.P256DH},
		"short auth":       {Endpoint: good.Endpoint, P256DH: good.P256DH, Auth: good.Auth[:15]},
		"long auth":        {Endpoint: good.Endpoint, P256DH: good.P256DH, Auth: append(bytes.Clone(good.Auth), 0)},
	} {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			body, err := Encrypt(sub, []byte("x"))
			assert.Error(t, err)
			assert.Nil(t, body)
		})
	}

	_, err := Encrypt(good, []byte("x"))
	assert.NoError(t, err, "control: the unmodified subscription encrypts")
}

func TestParseSubscription(t *testing.T) {
	good, _ := newSub(t)
	valid := core.PushSubscription{
		Endpoint: good.Endpoint,
		P256DH:   b64s(good.P256DH),
		Auth:     b64s(good.Auth),
	}

	got, err := ParseSubscription(valid)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, good, got)

	offCurve := bytes.Clone(good.P256DH)
	offCurve[len(offCurve)-1] ^= 0xff
	for name, mod := range map[string]func(s *core.PushSubscription){
		"empty endpoint":        func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.Endpoint = "" },
		"http endpoint":         func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.Endpoint = "http://web.push.apple.com/wpush/v2/abc" },
		"endpoint without host": func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.Endpoint = "https:///wpush/v2/abc" },
		"endpoint not a URL":    func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.Endpoint = "://nope" },
		"padded p256dh":         func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.P256DH = base64.URLEncoding.EncodeToString(good.P256DH) },
		"p256dh not base64":     func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.P256DH = "!!!" },
		"short p256dh":          func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.P256DH = b64s(good.P256DH[:64]) },
		"p256dh off curve":      func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.P256DH = b64s(offCurve) },
		"padded auth":           func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.Auth = base64.URLEncoding.EncodeToString(good.Auth) },
		"auth not base64":       func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.Auth = "!!!" },
		"short auth":            func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.Auth = b64s(good.Auth[:15]) },
		"long auth":             func(s *core.PushSubscription) { s.Auth = b64s(append(bytes.Clone(good.Auth), 0)) },
	} {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			s := valid
			mod(&s)
			got, err := ParseSubscription(s)
			assert.Error(t, err)
			assert.Equal(t, Subscription{}, got)
		})
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/web/webpush/encrypt.go`:

```go
// Package webpush builds Web Push requests: the aes128gcm body of RFC 8291,
// the VAPID authorization of RFC 8292 and the HTTP request of RFC 8030. It
// holds no state and does no network I/O, so whatever posts a request owns
// its timeout, its retries and the push service's answer.
package webpush

import (
	"crypto/aes"
	"crypto/cipher"
	"crypto/ecdh"
	"crypto/hkdf"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/binary"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/url"

	"github.com/aidan-bailey/loom/core"
)

const (
	saltLen    = 16
	authLen    = 16
	pointLen   = 65 // an uncompressed P-256 point
	tagLen     = 16 // AES-GCM's authentication tag
	recordSize = 4096
	// headerLen is the RFC 8188 header: the salt, the record size, the key
	// ID's length and the key ID, which RFC 8291 makes the sender's public key.
	headerLen = saltLen + 4 + 1 + pointLen
	// maxPayload is what one record of recordSize bytes holds beside the
	// header, the one padding delimiter and the tag: 3993 bytes.
	maxPayload   = recordSize - headerLen - 1 - tagLen
	paddingDelim = 0x02 // RFC 8188: the last record's delimiter
)

var errPayloadTooLarge = errors.New("webpush: payload does not fit one 4096-byte record")

// Subscription is a browser's push subscription, decoded.
type Subscription struct {
	Endpoint string
	P256DH   []byte // 65 bytes
	Auth     []byte // 16 bytes
}

// ParseSubscription decodes a stored subscription: an https endpoint, and the
// unpadded base64url key and secret, checked for their lengths and for the
// key being a point on P-256. Whether the endpoint's host is a push service
// is core.ValidatePushSubscription's check, made when the subscription is stored.
func ParseSubscription(s core.PushSubscription) (Subscription, error) {
	u, err := url.Parse(s.Endpoint)
	if err != nil || u.Scheme != "https" || u.Host == "" {
		return Subscription{}, errors.New("webpush: the endpoint is not an https URL")
	}
	p256dh, err := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(s.P256DH)
	if err != nil {
		return Subscription{}, fmt.Errorf("webpush: p256dh: %w", err)
	}
	auth, err := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(s.Auth)
	if err != nil {
		return Subscription{}, fmt.Errorf("webpush: auth: %w", err)
	}
	sub := Subscription{Endpoint: s.Endpoint, P256DH: p256dh, Auth: auth}
	if err := sub.check(); err != nil {
		return Subscription{}, err
	}
	return sub, nil
}

// check refuses a key or secret the encryption could not use.
func (s Subscription) check() error {
	if len(s.Auth) != authLen {
		return fmt.Errorf("webpush: auth is %d bytes, want %d", len(s.Auth), authLen)
	}
	if len(s.P256DH) != pointLen {
		return fmt.Errorf("webpush: p256dh is %d bytes, want %d", len(s.P256DH), pointLen)
	}
	if _, err := ecdh.P256().NewPublicKey(s.P256DH); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("webpush: p256dh is not a P-256 point: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

// Encrypt encrypts payload for sub as one RFC 8291 aes128gcm record with a
// record size of 4096: a fresh sender key pair and salt for every message, so
// two messages never share a key. The result is the request body, 86 bytes of
// header then the record. A payload over 3993 bytes is refused, since a push
// service need not take a body over 4096.
func Encrypt(sub Subscription, payload []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	sender, err := ecdh.P256().GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("webpush: sender key: %w", err)
	}
	salt := make([]byte, saltLen)
	if _, err := io.ReadFull(rand.Reader, salt); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("webpush: salt: %w", err)
	}
	return encryptWith(sub, payload, sender, salt)
}

// encryptWith is Encrypt with its random inputs given, so the tests can feed
// it RFC 8291's example.
func encryptWith(sub Subscription, payload []byte, sender *ecdh.PrivateKey, salt []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	if len(payload) > maxPayload {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: %d bytes, at most %d", errPayloadTooLarge, len(payload), maxPayload)
	}
	if len(salt) != saltLen {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("webpush: salt is %d bytes, want %d", len(salt), saltLen)
	}
	if err := sub.check(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	receiver, err := ecdh.P256().NewPublicKey(sub.P256DH)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("webpush: p256dh: %w", err)
	}
	secret, err := sender.ECDH(receiver)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("webpush: key agreement: %w", err)
	}
	senderPub := sender.PublicKey().Bytes()
	cek, nonce, err := contentKeys(secret, sub.Auth, sub.P256DH, senderPub, salt)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	block, err := aes.NewCipher(cek)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("webpush: cipher: %w", err)
	}
	gcm, err := cipher.NewGCM(block)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("webpush: cipher: %w", err)
	}

	body := make([]byte, 0, headerLen+len(payload)+1+tagLen)
	body = append(body, salt...)
	body = binary.BigEndian.AppendUint32(body, recordSize)
	body = append(body, byte(len(senderPub)))
	body = append(body, senderPub...)

	record := make([]byte, 0, len(payload)+1)
	record = append(record, payload...)
	record = append(record, paddingDelim)
	return gcm.Seal(body, nonce, record, nil), nil
}

// contentKeys derives the content encryption key and nonce: RFC 8291
// section 3.3 combines the ECDH secret with the authentication secret into
// the input keying material, then RFC 8188 expands that with the salt. The
// HKDF steps append the 0x01 counter byte themselves. The nonce is not
// XORed with a record number, since the one record is number zero.
func contentKeys(ecdhSecret, auth, uaPublic, asPublic, salt []byte) (cek, nonce []byte, err error) {
	keyInfo := make([]byte, 0, len("WebPush: info\x00")+len(uaPublic)+len(asPublic))
	keyInfo = append(keyInfo, "WebPush: info\x00"...)
	keyInfo = append(keyInfo, uaPublic...)
	keyInfo = append(keyInfo, asPublic...)
	ikm, err := hkdf.Key(sha256.New, ecdhSecret, auth, string(keyInfo), 32)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("webpush: input keying material: %w", err)
	}
	cek, err = hkdf.Key(sha256.New, ikm, salt, "Content-Encoding: aes128gcm\x00", 16)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("webpush: content encryption key: %w", err)
	}
	nonce, err = hkdf.Key(sha256.New, ikm, salt, "Content-Encoding: nonce\x00", 12)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("webpush: nonce: %w", err)
	}
	return cek, nonce, nil
}
```

```go
// RFC 8292, section 2.4: the JWT's first two segments for these claims, and
// the key that signed the whole token.
const (
	rfc8292Header = "eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJFUzI1NiJ9"
	rfc8292Claims = "eyJhdWQiOiJodHRwczovL3B1c2guZXhhbXBsZS5uZXQiLCJleHAiOjE0NTM1MjM3NjgsInN1YiI6Im1haWx0bzpwdXNoQGV4YW1wbGUuY29tIn0"
)

// newVAPIDKey makes a VAPID key the way the model does: the 32-byte scalar
// and the 65-byte uncompressed point.
func newVAPIDKey(t *testing.T) core.VAPIDKey {
	t.Helper()
	k, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	priv, err := k.Bytes()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	pub, err := k.PublicKey.Bytes()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return core.VAPIDKey{Private: priv, Public: pub}
}

// splitAuth cuts an Authorization value "vapid t=<JWT>, k=<key>" into its parts.
func splitAuth(t *testing.T, header string) (jwt, k string) {
	t.Helper()
	rest, ok := strings.CutPrefix(header, "vapid t=")
	require.True(t, ok, header)
	jwt, k, ok = strings.Cut(rest, ", k=")
	require.True(t, ok, header)
	return jwt, k
}

// verifyJWT checks a compact ES256 JWT against a public key, as a push
// service does, and returns its claims.
func verifyJWT(t *testing.T, jwt string, pub []byte) (vapidClaims, bool) {
	t.Helper()
	parts := strings.Split(jwt, ".")
	require.Len(t, parts, 3, jwt)
	sig := b64(t, parts[2])
	require.Len(t, sig, 64, "ES256 signatures are r then s, 32 bytes each")
	pk, err := ecdsa.ParseUncompressedPublicKey(elliptic.P256(), pub)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	der, err := asn1.Marshal(struct{ R, S *big.Int }{new(big.Int).SetBytes(sig[:32]), new(big.Int).SetBytes(sig[32:])})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	digest := sha256.Sum256([]byte(parts[0] + "." + parts[1]))
	var claims vapidClaims
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(b64(t, parts[1]), &claims))
	return claims, ecdsa.VerifyASN1(pk, digest[:], der)
}

func TestVAPIDSigningInput_MatchesRFC8292(t *testing.T) {
	got, err := vapidSigningInput("https://push.example.net", "mailto:push@example.com", 1453523768)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, rfc8292Header+"."+rfc8292Claims, got)
}

func TestVAPIDAuth_SignsClaimsThatVerify(t *testing.T) {
	key := newVAPIDKey(t)
	now := time.Unix(1_700_000_000, 0)
	for name, tc := range map[string]struct{ endpoint, aud string }{
		"path and query dropped": {"https://web.push.apple.com/wpush/v2/abc?x=1", "https://web.push.apple.com"},
		"port kept":              {"https://fcm.googleapis.com:8443/fcm/send/abc", "https://fcm.googleapis.com:8443"},
		"wildcard host":          {"https://eu.notify.windows.com/w/?token=abc", "https://eu.notify.windows.com"},
	} {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			header, err := VAPIDAuth(key, tc.endpoint, "https://loom.example.com", now)
			require.NoError(t, err)
			jwt, k := splitAuth(t, header)
			assert.Equal(t, b64s(key.Public), k, "k is the public key, base64url")
			assert.True(t, strings.HasPrefix(jwt, rfc8292Header+"."), "the header is typ JWT, alg ES256")

			claims, ok := verifyJWT(t, jwt, key.Public)
			assert.True(t, ok, "the signature verifies with the public key")
			assert.Equal(t, vapidClaims{
				Aud: tc.aud,
				Exp: 1_700_000_000 + 12*3600,
				Sub: "https://loom.example.com",
			}, claims)
		})
	}
}

func TestVAPIDAuth_SignatureBindsTheClaimsAndTheKey(t *testing.T) {
	key, other := newVAPIDKey(t), newVAPIDKey(t)
	header, err := VAPIDAuth(key, "https://web.push.apple.com/wpush/v2/abc", "https://loom.example.com", time.Unix(1_700_000_000, 0))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	jwt, _ := splitAuth(t, header)
	parts := strings.Split(jwt, ".")

	_, ok := verifyJWT(t, jwt, key.Public)
	require.True(t, ok, "control: the token verifies with its own key")

	_, ok = verifyJWT(t, jwt, other.Public)
	assert.False(t, ok, "another key")

	forged, err := json.Marshal(vapidClaims{Aud: "https://web.push.apple.com", Exp: 1_700_000_000 + 24*3600, Sub: "https://loom.example.com"})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, ok = verifyJWT(t, parts[0]+"."+b64s(forged)+"."+parts[2], key.Public)
	assert.False(t, ok, "claims changed after signing")
}

func TestVAPIDAuth_RefusesWhatItCannotSign(t *testing.T) {
	key, other := newVAPIDKey(t), newVAPIDKey(t)
	const endpoint = "https://web.push.apple.com/wpush/v2/abc"
	const subject = "https://loom.example.com"
	now := time.Unix(1_700_000_000, 0)

	_, err := VAPIDAuth(key, endpoint, subject, now)
	require.NoError(t, err, "control: the unmodified inputs sign")
	_, err = VAPIDAuth(key, endpoint, "mailto:me@example.com", now)
	require.NoError(t, err, "control: a mailto subject is fine")

	for name, tc := range map[string]struct {
		key               core.VAPIDKey
		endpoint, subject string
	}{
		"no private key":      {core.VAPIDKey{Public: key.Public}, endpoint, subject},
		"short private key":   {core.VAPIDKey{Private: key.Private[:31], Public: key.Public}, endpoint, subject},
		"no public key":       {core.VAPIDKey{Private: key.Private}, endpoint, subject},
		"public key of other": {core.VAPIDKey{Private: key.Private, Public: other.Public}, endpoint, subject},
		"empty subject":       {key, endpoint, ""},
		"subject not a URI":   {key, endpoint, "loom.example.com"},
		"subject over http":   {key, endpoint, "http://loom.example.com"},
		"subject over ftp":    {key, endpoint, "ftp://loom.example.com"},
		"empty endpoint":      {key, "", subject},
		"relative endpoint":   {key, "/wpush/v2/abc", subject},
		"endpoint over ftp":   {key, "ftp://web.push.apple.com/x", subject},
		"endpoint no host":    {key, "https:///x", subject},
	} {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			header, err := VAPIDAuth(tc.key, tc.endpoint, tc.subject, now)
			assert.Error(t, err)
			assert.Empty(t, header)
		})
	}
}
```
